(5.71)

Then

\displaystyle A(N)+\tilde{A}(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.72)

where the implicit constant is independent of the interval
I
.

The proof of Proposition 5.26 is based on the following facts and Proposition 5.27 below.

By Sobolev embedding, for a small parameter
\eta>0
to be chosen later, there exists such that
c=c(\eta)

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\leq\eta.

(5.73)

By the results of Lemma 5.24 and 5.25, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim A(N),

(5.74)

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim A(N)^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}A(N)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.75)

By the results of Lemma 5.5 and Lemma 5.20, we know that

\displaystyle[A(N)]^{2}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{2}dt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{2}dt.

(5.76)

While by the results of Proposition 5.22 and using Berstein’s estimates, we get

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{q}(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}u_{\geq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}P_{\geq N}(|v|^{4}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}P_{\geq N}(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+[\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}\left(\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{2}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.77)

Consequently,

\displaystyle A(N)+\tilde{A}_{q}(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad{\rm whenever}\quad N\geq\left(\frac{\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{2}dt}{\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}]^{-1}dt}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}.

(5.78)

Proposition 5.27(Recurrence relations for
A(N)
and
\tilde{A}_{q}(N)
).

For
\eta
sufficient small and
c=c(\eta)
is as in (
5.73
),

\displaystyle A(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta^{2}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N),

(5.79)

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{q}(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta A(N)+\eta^{2}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N),

(5.80)

uniformly in
N\in 2^{\mathbb{Z}}
.

Proof:
The recurrence relations for
A(N)
and
\tilde{A}_{q}(N)
rely on the Strichartz inequality in Lemma 5.20 and maximal Strichartz estimate in Proposition 5.22.
To deal with the contributions of the nonlinearities
|v|^{4}u
and
|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v
, we use the notation
\varnothing(X)
to denote a quantity that resembles
X
, and write

\displaystyle|v|^{4}u=\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)+\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u)+\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u),

(5.81)

\displaystyle|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v=\varnothing(u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3})+\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}v)+\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u).

(5.82)

Using the Strichartz inequality in Lemma 5.20, by(
5.81
), (
5.82
) and Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle A(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\nabla\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle:=1+(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)+(VI).

(5.83)

Using maximal Strichartz estimate in Proposition 5.22, by (
5.81
), (
5.82
) and Bernstein’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{q}(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}u_{\geq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}P_{\geq N}\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}}P_{\geq N}\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\nabla\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle:=1+(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)+(VI).

(5.84)

Before we consider these terms individually, we would like to point out that the terms (I)–(VI) in (
5.83
) are the same as those in (
5.84
), and we will show some facts below.

Fact 1: By Sobolev embedding,

\displaystyle\|u(t,x)\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim\|u(t,x)\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}},\quad\|v(t,x)\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim\|v(t,x)\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad t\in I.

(5.85)

Fact 2: Using Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle|u||v|^{2}\lesssim|u|^{3}+|v|^{3}.

(5.86)

In order to estimate (I), we decompose the time interval
I
into characteristic subintervals
J_{k}
where
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv\widetilde{N}_{k}
. On each these
subintervals, using Hölder’s inequality, Bernstein’s inequality, and Strichartz estimate for the admissible pair
(4,3)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}(J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\lesssim\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}(|u|^{3}+|v|^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}(J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}(J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}[\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}_{k}\|\nabla v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}(J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}_{k}.

Recalling that
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}
on
J_{k}
and
|J_{k}|\sim N_{k}^{-2}
, we can square and sum the estimates above over the subintervals
J_{k}
, and obtain

\displaystyle(I)=N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.87)

Similarly,

\displaystyle(IV)=N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}c^{-\frac{3}{2}}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.88)

To estimate (II), we recall (
5.86
) and point out

\displaystyle|v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>N}u|\leq|v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v_{>N}v_{>N}u|+|v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v_{>N}v_{\leq N}u|:=(II1)+(II2).

(5.89)

Using Berstein’s inequality and Schur’s test, for
6<q<\infty
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\|v_{>N}v_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\sum_{M_{1}\geq M_{2}\geq M_{3},M_{2}>N}\|v_{M_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{M_{2}}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}\|u_{M_{3}}\|_{L^{\frac{6q}{q-6}}_{x}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\sup_{M>N}\|M^{\frac{3}{q}-1}v_{M}(t)\|_{L^{q}_{x}}\sum_{M_{1}\geq M_{3}}(\frac{M_{3}}{M_{1}})^{\frac{3}{q}}\|\nabla v_{M_{1}}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla u_{M_{3}}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N).

(5.90)

Using Hölder’s inequality, by (
5.73
) and (
5.90
), we have

\displaystyle(II1)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{2}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{>N}v_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}\eta^{2}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N).

(5.91)

About (II2), using Hölder’s inequality and Berstein’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|v_{>N}v_{\leq N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{>N}|^{\frac{3}{2}}[|v_{\leq N}|^{3}+|u|^{3}]dx\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{>N}|^{3}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|v_{\leq N}|^{6}+|u|^{6}]dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{>N}|^{3}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|v_{\leq N}|^{6}+|u|^{6}]dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\sup_{M>N}M^{\frac{3}{q}-1}\|u_{M}(t)\|_{L^{q}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N).

(5.92)

Consequently, we get

\displaystyle(II)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N).

(5.93)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle(V)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}\tilde{A}_{q}(2N).

(5.94)

Last, by (
5.73
), (
5.74
) and (
5.75
), we have

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle=\|\nabla\varnothing(v^{2}_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}[\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta A(N).

(5.95)

Similarly,

\displaystyle(IV)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta A(N).

(5.96)

Putting (
5.87
)–(
5.96
) together, we can get (
5.79
) and (
5.80
).
\Box

Proof of Proposition 5.26:
Taking
\eta
small enough, using (
5.78
) and the results of Proposition 5.27, after a straightforward induction argument, we can obtain (
5.72
).
\Box

5.4
Impossibility of rapid frequency cascades

In this subsection, we prove that the first type of almost periodic solution described in Proposition 5.6 which satisfies
\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty
cannot exist.

Lemma 5.28(Finite mass).

Assume that
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
is an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,2)
satisfying

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}=+\infty\quad{\rm and}\quad K:=\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}N(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty.

(5.97)

Then
(u,v)\in[L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}]\times[L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}]
and for all
0<N<1
,

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{N}\left\{\sum_{M<N}\|\nabla u_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|\nabla v_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}1.

(5.98)

Proof:
First, we would like to point out that if (
5.98
) holds, then letting
N\rightarrow 0
in (
5.98
), using
\nabla u,\nabla v\in L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}
and Berstein’s inequality for high frequencies, we have

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\leq\|u_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|u_{>1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{>1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1,

(5.99)

which implies the finiteness of the mass.

Since
K
is finite, we extend the conclusions of Proposition 5.26 and Proposition 5.27 to the time interval
[0,T_{\max})
and find that
the second summand in (
5.98
) is
N^{-1}A(\frac{N}{2})
. Therefore, by Proposition 5.26 and Proposition 5.27 and Berstein’s inequality,

\displaystyle LHS(\ref{962})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}[\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+A(\frac{N}{2})]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}<+\infty.

(5.100)

Fixing
0<N<1
, using Duhamel formulae and Strichartz’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle LHS(\ref{962})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}(|v|^{4}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}(|v|^{4}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle:=(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV).

(5.101)

Before we estimate the contributions of (I)–(IV) to (
5.101
), we would like to point that

\displaystyle||v|^{4}u|+||u|^{2}|v|^{2}u|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)|+|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{<N}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})|

\displaystyle\quad+|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{>1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})|+|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})|

\displaystyle\quad+|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{\leq 1}u_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})|+|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>1}u_{\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})|

\displaystyle:=(i)+(ii1)+(ii2)+(iii1)+(iii2)+(iv).

(5.102)

Using Bernstein’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality, similar to (
5.87
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(N^{\frac{1}{2}}+1)\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}c^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.103)

Similarly, we consider the contribution of (ii1) and get

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{<N}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{<N}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{1}{N}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{<N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u_{<N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla v_{<N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}[\|u_{<N}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{<N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u_{<N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\times[\|\nabla u_{<N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta LHS(\ref{962}).

(5.104)

The contribution of (ii2) is entirely similar to that of (ii1).

Using Berstein’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality again, we consider the contribution of (iii1) and get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{<N}u_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v_{\leq 1}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|v_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+K^{\frac{1}{2}})LHS(\ref{962}).

(5.105)

The contribution of (iii2) is entirely similar to that of (iii1).

Similarly, we consider the contribution of (iv) and get

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{N}\|\nabla P_{<N}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>1}u_{\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{N\leq\cdot\leq 1}\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>1}u_{\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(N^{\frac{1}{2}}+1)\|\varnothing(v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}v^{2}_{>1}u_{\leq 1}v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>1}u_{\leq 1})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+K^{\frac{1}{2}}).

(5.106)

Coming back to (
5.101
), thanks to the results of (
5.102
)–(
5.106
), we have

\displaystyle(I)+(II)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+c^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta(1+K^{\frac{1}{2}})LHS(\ref{962}).

(5.107)

Similarly,

\displaystyle(III)+(IV)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+c^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta(1+K^{\frac{1}{2}})LHS(\ref{962}).

(5.108)

(
5.107
) and (
5.108
) mean that

LHS(\ref{962})\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+c^{-\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}+\eta(1+K^{\frac{1}{2}})LHS(\ref{962}).

If we chose
\eta
small enough, we can get (
5.98
).
\Box

Now we are ready to prove the main result in this subsection.

Propostion 5.29(No rapid frequency-cascades).

There are no almost periodic solution
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,2)
satisfying

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}=+\infty,\quad\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty.

(5.109)

Proof:
Assume contradictorily that
(u,v)
is such a solution. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{t\rightarrow T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)=+\infty.

(5.110)

Using Strichartz inequality and Bernstein’s inequality

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|P_{\leq N}(|v|^{4}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|P_{\leq N}(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(|v|^{4}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+N^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}.

(5.111)

Young’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle||v|^{4}u|+|(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v|\lesssim|u^{5}|+|v^{5}|.

(5.112)

If we write

u^{5}=\varnothing(u^{3}_{\leq 1}u^{2})+\varnothing(u^{3}_{>1}u^{2}),\quad v^{5}=\varnothing(v^{3}_{\leq 1}v^{2})+\varnothing(v^{3}_{>1}v^{2}),

we have

\displaystyle\quad\|u^{5}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|v^{5}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\varnothing(u^{3}_{\leq 1}u^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u^{3}_{>1}u^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(v^{3}_{\leq 1}v^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(v^{3}_{>1}v^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|u_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|u_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|u^{3}_{>1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|v_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|v_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\|v\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|v^{3}_{>1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}1.

This estimate above, (
5.111
) and (
5.112
) imply that

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.113)

Recalling that for any
\eta>0
there exists
c=c(u,v,\eta)>0
such that

\|\nabla u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta,

we get

\displaystyle\|u(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|v(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|P_{>N}u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|u_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|P_{>N}v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{>c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{-1}\|\nabla u_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+c^{-1}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+N^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{-1}\|\nabla v_{\leq c\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+c^{-1}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{-1}\eta+c^{-1}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}.

Using (
5.110
), we can make the right-hand side here as small as we wish, which implies that
\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}=0
.
Consequently,
(u,v)\equiv(0,0)
, which is a contradiction to the hypothesis
\|u\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}}+\|v\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}}=+\infty
.
\Box

5.5
The frequency-localized weight-coupled interaction Morawetz inequality

In this subsection, we give a spacetime bound on the high-frequency portion of the solution.

Proposition 5.30(A frequency-localized interaction Morawetz estimate).

Assume that
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
is an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,2)
such that
\widetilde{N}(t)\geq 1
. Let
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
be a union of contiguous characteristic intervals
J_{k}
. Fix
0<\eta_{0}\leq 1
. Then for sufficiently small
N>0
which depends on
\eta_{0}
but not on
I
,

\displaystyle\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|u_{>N}(t,x)|^{4}+|v_{>N}(t,x)|^{4}]dxdt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta_{0}(N^{-3}+K),

(5.114)

where the implicit constant in the inequality above does not depend on
\eta_{0}
or the interval
I
,
K:=\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt
.

We also introduce the weight-coupled interaction Morawetz identity below.

Proposition 5.31.

Assume that

\displaystyle i\phi_{t}+\Delta\phi=\lambda|\psi|^{4}\phi+\mathcal{F}_{1},\quad i\psi_{t}+\Delta\psi=\mu|\phi|^{2}|\psi|^{2}\psi+\mathcal{F}_{2},

(5.115)

for some weight
a:\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, we define the following weight-coupled Morawetz interaction:

\displaystyle M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)

\displaystyle=2A\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,x)\bar{\phi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\phi(t,x)\phi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2B\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\psi}(t,x)\bar{\psi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\psi(t,x)\psi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,x)\bar{\psi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\phi(t,x)\psi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2D\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\psi}(t,x)\bar{\phi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\psi(t,x)\phi(t,y))]dxdy,

(5.116)

where
\widetilde{\nabla}=(\nabla_{x},\nabla_{y})
,
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, the constants
A
,
B
,
C
and
D
are defined as in (
4.4
). Then

\displaystyle\qquad\frac{d}{dt}M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)

\displaystyle\thicksim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-[A|\phi|^{2}+D|\psi|^{2}]\Delta_{x}\Delta_{x}a+4a_{jk}Re(A\phi_{j}\bar{\phi}_{k}+D\psi_{j}\bar{\psi}_{k})+L_{1}|\phi|^{2}|\psi|^{4}\Delta_{x}a\right)dx|\phi(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-[A|\phi|^{2}+C|\psi|^{2}]\Delta_{y}\Delta_{y}a+4a_{jk}Re(A\phi_{j}\bar{\phi}_{k}+C\psi_{j}\bar{\psi}_{k})+L_{2}|\phi|^{2}|\psi|^{4}\Delta_{y}a\right)dy|\phi(t,x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-[B|\psi|^{2}+C|\phi|^{2}]\Delta_{x}\Delta_{x}a+4a_{jk}Re(B\psi_{j}\bar{\psi}_{k}+C\phi_{j}\bar{\phi}_{k})+L_{3}|\phi|^{2}|\psi|^{4}\Delta_{x}a\right)dx|\psi(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-[B|\psi|^{2}+D|\phi|^{2}]\Delta_{y}\Delta_{y}a+4a_{jk}Re(B\psi_{j}\bar{\psi}_{k}+D\phi_{j}\bar{\phi}_{k})+L_{4}|\phi|^{2}|\psi|^{4}\Delta_{y}a\right)dy|\psi(t,x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle-4\sum_{m=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)]dx[\nabla_{y}\cdot(\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,y)\nabla_{y}\phi(t,y)+\bar{\psi}(t,y)\nabla_{y}\psi(t,y)])]dy

\displaystyle-4\sum_{m=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{y}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,y)\nabla_{y}w_{m}(t,y)]dy[\nabla_{x}\cdot(\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,x)\nabla_{x}\phi(t,x)+\bar{\psi}(t,x)\nabla_{x}\psi(t,x)])])]dx

\displaystyle+4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Re(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}\nabla_{x}w_{m}-\bar{w}_{m}\nabla_{x}\mathcal{F}_{m})\cdot\nabla_{x}adx|w_{n}(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle+4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Re(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}\nabla_{y}w_{m}-\bar{w}_{m}\nabla_{y}\mathcal{F}_{m})\cdot\nabla_{y}ady|w_{n}(t,x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle-4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)]dx\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,y)dy

\displaystyle-4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{y}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,y)\nabla_{y}w_{m}(t,y)]dy\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,x)dx.

(5.117)

Here
w_{1}
,
w_{2}
can be taken as follows: (1) one is
\phi
, another is
\psi
; (2) both are
\phi
; (3) both are
\psi
. And the constants
L_{1}
,
L_{2}
,
L_{3}
and
L_{4}
are defined as in (
4.5
).

Proof:
(
5.117
) can be obtained by (
5.116
) after some elementary computations, we omit the details here. However,
we take “
\frac{d}{dt}M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)\thicksim
” but not “
\frac{d}{dt}M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)=
” because the coefficients in some terms on the righthand are different by the multiple of some constants.
\Box

Similar to the discussions in
[
55
]
, we choose
a
be a smooth spherically symmetric function satisfying

\displaystyle a(0)=0,\quad a_{r}\geq 0,\quad a_{rr}\leq 0,\quad|\partial_{r}^{k}a_{r}|\lesssim_{k}J^{-1}r^{-k}\quad{\rm for\ each}\quad k\geq 1,

(5.118)

and

a_{r}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{llll}&1,\quad r\leq R,\\
&1-\frac{\log(\frac{r}{R})}{J},\quad eR\leq r\leq e^{J-J_{0}}R,\\
&0,\qquad e^{J}R\leq r,\\
\end{array}\right.

(5.119)

where
J_{0}\geq 1
,
J\geq 2J_{0}
and
R
are parameters which will be determined in due course. Moreover,
a(x)=|x|
if
|x|\leq R
,
a(x)
is a constant if
|x|\geq e^{J}R
, while

\displaystyle\frac{2}{r}a_{r}\geq\frac{2J_{0}}{Jr},\quad|a_{rr}|\leq\frac{1}{Jr}\quad{\rm if}\quad eR\leq r\leq e^{J-J_{0}}R.

(5.120)

For a parameter
N
such that

\displaystyle e^{J}RN=1,

(5.121)

we will apply Proposition 5.31 with

\displaystyle\phi=u_{h_{i}}:=u_{>N},\quad\mathcal{F}_{1}=P_{hi}(|v|^{4}u)-|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi},

(5.122)

\displaystyle\psi=v_{h_{i}}:=v_{>N},\quad\mathcal{F}_{2}=P_{hi}(|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v)-|u_{hi}|^{2}|v_{hi}|^{2}v_{hi}.

(5.123)

(By the way, we also write
u_{lo}:=u_{\leq N}
and
v_{lo}:=v_{\leq N}
.) Since
(u,v)
is almost periodic modulo symmetries and
\widetilde{N}(t)\geq 1
, if we take
R
large enough, then
N
can be small such that for given
\eta=\eta(\eta_{0},u,v)
,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|\nabla u_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}+|Nu_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|Nv_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]dx

\displaystyle+\int_{|x-x(t)|>\frac{R}{2}}[|\nabla u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]dx<\eta^{2}

(5.124)

uniformly for
0\leq t<T_{\max}
.

Corollary 5.32(A priori bounds).

Under the assumption (
5.124
),

\displaystyle\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}.

(5.125)

For all admissible pair
(q,r)
, i.e.,
\frac{2}{q}+\frac{3}{r}=\frac{3}{2}
, and any
s<1-\frac{3}{q}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|N^{1-s}|\nabla|^{s}u_{hi}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|N^{1-s}|\nabla|^{s}v_{hi}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{q}}.

(5.126)

For any
\rho\leq Re^{J}=N^{-1}
,

\displaystyle\int_{I}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{|x-y|\leq\rho}[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]dydt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\rho(K+N^{-3}).

(5.127)

Proof:
(
5.125
) is the direct results of Proposition 5.26 and (
5.75
). When
q=2
, the bound of
\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}
in (
5.126
) can be controlled similarly. If
q\neq 2
, using the conversation of energy, it can be deduced by interpolation.

To estimate
u_{hi}
and
v_{hi}
, recalling that

A(M):=\left\{\sum_{M^{\prime}\leq M}[\|\nabla u_{M^{\prime}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|\nabla v_{M^{\prime}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}]\right\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}(1+M^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}

uniformly in
M
, and by Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad M^{1-s}\||\nabla|^{s}u_{M}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+M^{1-s}\||\nabla|^{s}v_{M}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla u_{M}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{M}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}A(M)^{\frac{2}{q}}[\|\nabla u\|^{\frac{(q-2)}{q}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v\|^{\frac{(q-2)}{q}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}(1+M^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{q}},

multiply through by
M^{s-1}
, and sum over
M\geq N
, then noticing
\frac{3}{q}+s<1
, we can guarantee the convergence of this sum and obtain the estimates for the parts containing
u_{hi}
and
v_{hi}
in (
5.126
).

To establish (
5.127
), we write

\displaystyle|v|^{4}u=\varnothing(v^{2}_{>N}v^{2}u)+\varnothing(u_{>N}v_{>N}v^{3})+\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v^{3}_{\leq N}v)+\varnothing(v^{4}_{\leq N}u)

\displaystyle|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v=\varnothing(u^{2}_{>N}v^{3})+\varnothing(u_{>N}v_{>N}v^{2}u)+\varnothing(v^{2}_{>N}u^{2}v)+\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v^{3}_{\leq N}u)+\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq N}v^{2}_{\leq N}v).

Recalling Proposition 5.26, Proposition 5.27 and (
5.83
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>N}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u_{>N}v_{>N}v^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v^{3}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(v^{4}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u^{2}_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|u^{2}_{>N}v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|v^{2}_{>N}v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|v^{2}_{>N}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}+K^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.128)

\displaystyle\quad\||u|^{2}|v|^{2}v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}}+K^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.129)

and by Bernstein’s inequality, (
5.75
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\|\varnothing(u^{2}_{>N}v^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u_{>N}v_{>N}v^{2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(v^{2}_{>N}u^{2}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v^{3}_{\leq N}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}+\|\varnothing(u^{2}_{\leq N}v^{2}_{\leq N}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}[\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}+N^{\frac{1}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.130)

\displaystyle\quad\||v|^{4}u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}+N^{\frac{1}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.131)

Combing (
5.128
)–(
5.131
) with the results of Proposition 5.23, we obtain

\displaystyle\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|\mathcal{S}u_{>N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\mathcal{S}v_{>N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}+(N^{\frac{1}{2}}+\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}})(K+N^{-3})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.132)

Since
\rho\leq Re^{J}=N^{-1}
, modulo a factor of
\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}
,
(\mathcal{S}u_{>N}(t,x),\mathcal{S}u_{>N}(t,x))
controls the
L^{2}_{x}
-norm on the ball around
x
,
(
5.127
) can be deduced by (
5.132
).
\Box

Lemma 5.33(Mass-mass interactions).

\displaystyle\quad 16\pi[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}]

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a_{jjkk}(x-y)[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}][|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{\eta^{2}e^{2J}}{J}(K+N^{-3}).

(5.133)

Proof:
Noticing that
\Delta|x|=2|x|^{-1}
and
-\Delta(4\pi|x|)^{-1}=\delta(x)
, we only to estimate the error terms originating from the truncation of
a
at radii
|x-y|\geq R
. By (
5.118
), (
5.121
), (
5.124
) and (
5.127
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a_{jjkk}(x-y)[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}][|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}J^{-1}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\sum_{j=0}^{J}(Re^{j})^{-2}(K+N^{-3})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{\eta^{2}e^{2J}}{J}(K+N^{-3}).\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\Box

Denote

\displaystyle B_{I}

\displaystyle:=\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\leq e^{J-J_{0}}R}|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{4}\Delta_{x}adx[L_{1}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{3}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)dy

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\leq e^{J-J_{0}}R}|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{4}\Delta_{y}ady[L_{2}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{4}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)dx,

(5.134)

\displaystyle B_{II}

\displaystyle:=\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\geq e^{J-J_{0}}R}|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{4}|\Delta_{x}a|dx[L_{1}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{3}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)dy

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\geq e^{J-J_{0}}R}|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{4}|\Delta_{y}a|dy[L_{2}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{4}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)dx,

(5.135)

Lemma 5.34.

Let
B_{I}
and
B_{II}
be defined as (
5.134
) and (
5.135
). Then

\displaystyle B_{I}\geq 0,\quad B_{II}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{J_{0}^{2}}{J}(K+N^{-3}).

(5.136)

Proof:

B_{I}\geq 0
is a direct result of
\Delta_{x}a\geq 0
and
\Delta_{y}a\geq 0
by the construction of
a
. Noticing that
|\Delta_{x}a|\lesssim J_{0}(Jr)^{-1}
and
|\Delta_{y}a|\lesssim J_{0}(Jr)^{-1}
when
r\geq e^{J-J_{0}}R
and
a_{kk}=0
when
r>e^{JR}
, we get

\displaystyle B_{II}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\geq e^{J-J_{0}}R}\frac{J_{0}}{J|x-y|}|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{4}dx[L_{1}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{3}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)dy

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\geq e^{J-J_{0}}R}\frac{J_{0}}{J|x-y|}|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{4}dy[L_{2}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{4}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{I}\int\int_{e^{J-J_{0}}R\leq|x-y|\leq e^{J}R}\frac{J_{0}}{J|x-y|}[|u_{hi}|^{6}+|v_{hi}|^{6}](t,x)dx[L_{1}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{3}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)dy

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int\int_{e^{J-J_{0}}R\leq|x-y|\leq e^{J}R}\frac{J_{0}}{J|x-y|}[|u_{hi}|^{6}+|v_{hi}|^{6}](t,y)dy[L_{2}|u_{hi}|^{2}+L_{4}|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}J_{0}[\|u_{hi}\|^{6}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{6}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]\sum_{j=J-J_{0}}^{J}(Je^{j}R)^{-1}(e^{j}R)(K+N^{-3})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{J^{2}_{0}}{J}(K+N^{-3}),

which completes the proof of (
5.136
).
\Box

Lemma 5.35.

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a_{jk}Re[(u_{hi})_{j}(\bar{u}_{hi})_{k}+(v_{hi})_{j}(\bar{v}_{hi})_{k}](t,x)dx[|u_{hi}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}a_{jk}Re[(u_{hi})_{j}(\bar{u}_{hi})_{k}+(v_{hi})_{j}(\bar{v}_{hi})_{k}](t,y)dy[|u_{hi}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(\eta^{2}+\frac{J_{0}}{J})(K+N^{-3})+\frac{1}{J_{0}}B_{I}.

(5.137)

Proof:
Since the
3\times 3
-matrix
(a_{jk})
is invariant under
x\leftrightarrow y
and real symmetric for any
x
and
y
, and
a_{jk}Re[(u_{hi})_{j}(\bar{u}_{hi})_{k}+(v_{hi})_{j}(\bar{v}_{hi})_{k}](t,x)[|u_{hi}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,y)
and
a_{jk}Re[(u_{hi})_{j}(\bar{u}_{hi})_{k}+(v_{hi})_{j}(\bar{v}_{hi})_{k}](t,y)[|u_{hi}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}](t,x)
define two positive semi-definite quadratic forms on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
for each
x
,
y
. Therefore, wherever
(a_{jk})
is positive semi-definite, (
5.137
) is true. By the choice of
a
, (
5.119
) and (
5.120
), we only to estimate

\displaystyle(I):=\int_{I}\int\int_{R<|x-y|<e^{J}R}\frac{[|\nabla u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}][u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]}{J|x-y|}dxdydt

(5.138)

and

\displaystyle(II):=\int_{I}\int\int_{R<|x-y|<e^{J}R}\frac{[|\nabla u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}][u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]}{J|x-y|}dxdydt.

(5.139)

We only estimate (
5.138
). (
5.139
) can be estimated similarly. We break the integral into two regions:
|x-x(t)|>\frac{R}{2}
and
|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{R}{2}
, the corresponding integrands are (I1) and (I2).

In the region
|x-x(t)|>\frac{R}{2}
, by (
5.124
) and (
5.127
), we have

\displaystyle(I1)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|\nabla u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(|x-x(t)|>\frac{R}{2})}+\|\nabla v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(|x-x(t)|>\frac{R}{2})}]\sum_{j=0}^{J}(Je^{j}R)^{-1}(e^{j}R)(K+N^{-3})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}(K+N^{-3}).

(5.140)

In the region
|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{R}{2}
, we further break the integral into two regions:
|x-y|>Re^{J-J_{0}}
and
|x-y|\leq Re^{J-J_{0}}
, the corresponding integrands are (I21) and (I22). In the region
|x-y|>Re^{J-J_{0}}
, similar to (
5.140
), we get

\displaystyle(I21)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|\nabla u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\sum_{j=J-J_{0}}^{J}(Je^{j}R)^{-1}(e^{j}R)(K+N^{-3})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{J_{0}}{J}(K+N^{-3}).

(5.141)

Since
(u,v)
is almost periodic,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|\nabla u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]dx\lesssim_{(u,v)}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}]dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{R}{2}}[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}]dx\quad{\rm uniformly\ for}\quad t\in[0,T_{\max}).

Since
\Delta a=a_{rr}+\frac{2}{r}a_{r}
in space dimension 3, recalling (
5.124
), (
5.127
) and
J_{0}\geq 1
, we know that the remaining integral is
\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{1}{J}B_{I}
.
\Box

Let

\displaystyle\tilde{{\mathcal{M}}}(u_{hi},v_{hi})

\displaystyle:=4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Re(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}\nabla_{x}w_{m}-\bar{w}_{m}\nabla_{x}\mathcal{F}_{m})\cdot\nabla_{x}adx|w_{n}(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\qquad+4\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Re(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}\nabla_{y}w_{m}-\bar{w}_{m}\nabla_{y}\mathcal{F}_{m})\cdot\nabla_{y}ady|w_{n}(t,x)|^{2}dx,

(5.142)

where
w_{m}
can be taken
u_{hi}
or
v_{hi}
, the same does to
w_{n}
. We have

Lemma 5.36.

For any
\epsilon\in(0,1]
,

\displaystyle|\int_{I}\tilde{{\mathcal{M}}}(u_{hi},v_{hi})dt|\lesssim_{(u,v)}\epsilon B_{I}+\eta[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]+(\epsilon^{-1}\eta+\epsilon\frac{J^{2}_{0}}{J})(N^{-3}+K).

(5.143)

Proof:
Denote

\displaystyle f_{1}(u,v)=|v|^{4}u,\quad f_{2}(u,v)=|u|^{2}|v|^{2}v,\quad\mathcal{F}_{1}=f_{1}(u,v)-P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v),\quad\mathcal{F}_{2}=f_{2}(u,v)-P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v).

Then

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{m=1}^{2}\Re(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}\nabla_{x}w_{m}-\bar{w}_{m}\nabla_{x}\mathcal{F}_{m})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}\varnothing(u_{hi}f_{1}+v_{hi}f_{2})+\varnothing(u_{hi}|v|^{4}\nabla_{x}u_{lo}+u_{hi}u|v|^{2}v\nabla_{x}v_{lo})

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing(v_{hi}|u|^{2}|v|^{2}\nabla_{x}v_{lo}+v_{hi}u|v|^{2}v\nabla_{x}u_{lo})+\nabla_{x}\varnothing(u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}+v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing(u_{hi}\nabla_{x}P_{lo}f_{1}+v_{hi}\nabla_{x}P_{lo}f_{2})

\displaystyle:=(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V).

(5.144)

And

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\tilde{{\mathcal{M}}}(u_{hi},v_{hi})dt

\displaystyle=\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\left[(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)\right](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt

\displaystyle+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\left[(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)\right](t,x)dx|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]dydt

\displaystyle+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{y}a\cdot\left[(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)\right](t,y)dy|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{y}a\cdot\left[(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)+(V)\right](t,y)dy|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}dxdt

\displaystyle:=(1)+(2)+(3)+(4).

(5.145)

We only estimate (1) in details, those of (2), (3) and (4) are similar to (1). First, we deal with

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\nabla_{x}a\cdot(I)](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt|

\displaystyle=|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Delta_{x}a\varnothing(u_{hi}f_{1}+v_{hi}f_{2})(t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\Delta_{x}a|[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{6}]|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}[|u_{hi}||v_{lo}|^{2}(|u_{hi}|+|v_{hi}|+|u_{lo}+|v_{lo}|)^{3}(t,x)]}{\epsilon|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}[|v_{hi}||u_{lo}|^{2}(|u_{hi}|+|v_{hi}|+|u_{lo}+|v_{lo}|)^{3}(t,x)]}{\epsilon|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{\epsilon J^{2}_{0}}{J}(K+N^{-3})+\frac{1}{\epsilon}\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}|u_{hi}|\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\epsilon}\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}|v_{hi}|\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{\epsilon J^{2}_{0}}{J}(N^{-3}+K)+\frac{1}{\epsilon}[\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\times[\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\frac{\epsilon J^{2}_{0}}{J}+\frac{\eta}{\epsilon}](N^{-3}+K).

(5.146)

Since
|\nabla_{x}a|
is uniformly bounded and

\displaystyle|(II)|+|(III)

\displaystyle|\lesssim[|u_{hi}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}][|u|^{3}+|v|^{3}][|\nabla_{x}u_{lo}|+|\nabla_{x}v_{lo}|]

\displaystyle\quad+[|u_{hi}|+|v_{hi}|][|u|^{2}+|v|^{2}][|u_{lo}|^{2}+|v_{lo}|^{2}][|\nabla_{x}u_{lo}|+|\nabla_{x}v_{lo}|],

(5.147)

we have

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot[(II)+(III)](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|[(II)+(III)]|(t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[|(II)|+|(III)|](t,x)dxdt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\eta^{2}N^{-2}[\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}][\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times[\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]+(\eta+\eta^{2})(N^{-3}+K).

(5.148)

Here we use
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u_{hi}|^{2}(t,y)dy\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}
by (
5.127
).

Now we deal the term containing (IV) by integrating by parts

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\nabla_{x}a\cdot(IV)](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt

\displaystyle=\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u_{hi}|^{2}(t,y)\Delta_{x}a\varnothing(u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}+v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2})(t,x)dxdydt.

(5.149)

Writing
u_{hi}(t,x)=div(\nabla\Delta^{-1}u_{hi}(t,x)
and
v_{hi}(t,x)=div(\nabla\Delta^{-1}v_{hi}(t,x)
, and integrating by parts once more, then applying the Mikhlin multiple theorem, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|u_{hi}|^{2}(t,y)\Delta_{x}a\varnothing(u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}+v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2})(t,x)dxdydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||x|^{-2}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||x|^{-2}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}.

(5.150)

Applying Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality to the first factor in each term above, and using Sobolev embedding on very last factors in the second term and the fourth one, we get

\displaystyle RHS(\ref{9104})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\||u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\||u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}.

(5.151)

We need to estimate
\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}
and
\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}
. Since
u=u_{hi}+u_{lo}
and
v=v_{hi}+v_{lo}
, we have

\displaystyle|v_{hi}+v_{lo}|^{4}(u_{hi}+u_{lo})=[u_{lo}+u_{hi}][|v_{lo}|^{4}+4v_{hi}|\xi_{1}|^{2}\xi_{1}]

(5.152)

\displaystyle|u_{hi}+u_{lo}|^{2}|v_{hi}+v_{lo}|^{2}(v_{hi}+v_{lo})=[|u_{lo}|^{2}+2u_{hi}\xi_{2}][|v_{lo}|^{2}v_{lo}+3v_{hi}|\xi_{3}|^{2}],

(5.153)

Here
\xi_{2}
is some function “between”
u_{lo}
and
u_{hi}+u_{lo}
, while
\xi_{1}
and
\xi_{3}
are some functions “between”
v_{lo}
and
v_{hi}+v_{lo}
. Therefore, we can write

\displaystyle f_{1}

\displaystyle=|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo}+\varnothing(u_{hi}|v|^{4})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u|\xi_{2}|^{2}\xi_{1}),

(5.154)

\displaystyle f_{2}

\displaystyle=|u_{lo}|^{2}|v_{lo}|^{2}v_{lo}+\varnothing(u_{hi}\xi_{2}|v|^{2}v)+\varnothing(v_{hi}|u|^{2}|\xi_{3}|^{2}).

(5.155)

Using Hölder’s, Bernetein’s and Young’s inequalities, we can get

\displaystyle\quad\|\nabla P_{lo}(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim N^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{4}}[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|u_{lo}\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}},

(5.156)

\displaystyle\quad\|\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}|v|^{4})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}|v|^{4}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{11}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}[\|u\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}},

(5.157)

and similarly,

\displaystyle\|\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(v_{hi}u|\xi_{1}|^{2}\xi_{1})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}},

(5.158)

\displaystyle\|\nabla P_{lo}|u_{lo}|^{2}|v_{lo}|^{2}v_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}},

(5.159)

\displaystyle\|\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}\xi_{2}|v|^{2}v)\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}},

(5.160)

\displaystyle\|\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(v_{hi}|u|^{2}|\xi_{3}|^{2})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}.

(5.161)

Putting (
5.149
)–(
5.161
) together, by the results of Corollary 5.32 and (
5.127
), using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\nabla_{x}a\cdot(IV)](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}[N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}]

\displaystyle\qquad+\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}[N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-1}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}[N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}]

\displaystyle\qquad+\eta N^{-1}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}[N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{\frac{3}{4}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+(N^{-3}+K)].

(5.162)

Now we deal with the term containing (V). Similarly, we also write
u_{hi}(t,x)=div(\nabla\Delta^{-1}u_{hi}(t,x)
and
v_{hi}(t,x)=div(\nabla\Delta^{-1}v_{hi}(t,x)
, and integrate by parts once, then

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\nabla_{x}a\cdot(V)](t,x)dx|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}dydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\nabla a)*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\nabla a)*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}v_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}.

(5.163)

The first and third terms in (
5.163
) have been estimated above. We estimate the second and fourth ones. By (
5.152
), we write

f_{1}=|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo}+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|v_{lo}|^{2})+\varnothing(u_{lo}v_{hi}|\xi_{1}|^{3})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}|\xi_{1}|^{3}).

Using Bernstein, Hölder and Young’s inequality, we can obtain

\displaystyle\quad\|\Delta P_{lo}(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\quad\|\Delta P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|v_{lo}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{2}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}[\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\quad\|\Delta P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{lo}v_{hi}|\xi_{1}|^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{5}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}[\|u_{lo}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+N^{2}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}[\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{5}{2}}\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\quad\|\Delta P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}|\xi_{1}|^{3})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\xi_{1}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}].

Here we use the fact that
|\xi_{1}|\lesssim|v|+|v_{hi}|+|v_{lo}|
. Consequently,

\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]

and

\displaystyle\|(\nabla a)*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]\right).

Similarly,

\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}].

and

\displaystyle\|(\nabla a)*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\Delta P_{lo}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(N(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]\right).

Combining all results above, we have estimated (1). Similar results on (2), (3) and (4) can also be obtained. Putting all the results together, the proof of this lemma is finished.
\Box

Now we deal with the mass bracket terms.

Lemma 5.37(Mass bracket terms).

\displaystyle|\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)]dx\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,y)dydt|

\displaystyle+|\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\nabla_{y}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,y)\nabla_{y}w_{m}(t,y)]dy\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,x)dxdt|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{4}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+(N^{-3}+K)].

(5.164)

Here
w_{m}
,
w_{n}
can be taken as follows: (1) one is
u_{hi}
, another is
v_{hi}
; (2) both are
u_{hi}
; (3) both are
v_{hi}
.

Proof:
First, we write

\displaystyle\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{1}u_{hi})

\displaystyle=\Im[u_{hi}P_{hi}(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo})]-\Im[u_{hi}P_{lo}(|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi})]+\Im[\varnothing(v_{lo}u^{2}_{hi}|\zeta_{1}|^{3})]+\Im[\varnothing(u_{lo}u_{hi}v_{hi}|\zeta_{1}|^{3})]

\displaystyle:=(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)

(5.165)

and consequently

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)]dx\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{1}u_{hi})(t,y)dydt

\displaystyle=\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)][(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV)]dxdydt.

(5.166)

We will treat their contributions in order.

Obviously,

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)][(I)(t,y)]dxdydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bar{w}_{m}\nabla w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\|u_{hi}P_{hi}(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}N^{-1}\|\nabla(|v_{lo}|^{4}u_{lo}))\|_{L^{1}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-3}[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}(N^{-3}+K).

(5.167)

For the term containing (II), we write
u_{hi}=div(\nabla\Delta^{-1}u_{hi})
and integrate by parts. Then we get

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)][(II)(t,y)]dxdydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bar{w}_{m}\nabla w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|\nabla P_{lo}(|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\||x|^{-1}*|\bar{w}_{m}\nabla w_{m}|\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-1}u_{hi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\|P_{lo}(|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}N^{\frac{3}{2}}\|(|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|w_{m}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}N^{\frac{3}{4}}\|(|v_{hi}|^{4}u_{hi})\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(N^{-3}+K)^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\|w_{m}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}N^{\frac{1}{4}}(N^{-3}+K)^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{15}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{15}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times[\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{1}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(\eta+\eta^{\frac{1}{4}})[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{-3}+K].

(5.168)

For the term containing (III), by the choice of
a
and
Re^{J}=N^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)][(III)(t,y)]dxdydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|w_{m}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}\|\nabla a\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times[\|u\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|u_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|u_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}](e^{J}R)^{\frac{3}{4}}\eta^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{-3}+K].

(5.169)

The term containing (IV) can be treated similarly, and

\displaystyle\quad|\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}a\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)][(IV)(t,y)]dxdydt|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{2}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+N^{-3}+K].

(5.170)

This completes the control of the mass bracket terms.
\Box

Proof of Proposition 5.29:
If we take
\phi=u_{hi}
,
\psi=v_{hi}
and the function
a
as above, and choosing

N
is small enough such that (
5.124
) holds, using Hölder’s inequality, we have

|M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)|\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|u_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}][\|\nabla u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{3}N^{-3}.

All the integral are on
I\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Putting all the conclusions of the above lemmas, we get

\displaystyle 16\pi[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]+B_{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(\epsilon+\frac{1}{J_{0}})B_{I}+\eta^{\frac{1}{4}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}(\eta^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\eta}{\epsilon}+\frac{J^{2}_{0}}{J}+\eta^{2}\frac{e^{2J}}{J})(N^{-3}+K).

(5.171)

Now we choose the parameters as follows: First, we choose
\epsilon+J^{-1}_{0}<<1
; Second, for these fixed
\epsilon
and
J^{-1}_{0}
, we choose
\eta<<1
and
J^{-}
small enough such that
(\eta^{\frac{1}{4}}+\frac{\eta}{\epsilon}+\frac{J^{2}_{0}}{J}+\eta^{2}\frac{e^{2J}}{J})<\eta_{0}
; Last, for the fixed
J
, we choose
R
and
N^{-1}
large enough such that
NRe^{J}=1
, yet we can increase
N^{-1}
or
R
such that (
5.124
) holds.

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\quad[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\||\nabla|^{\frac{1}{4}}u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{1}{4}}v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{3}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}+N^{-3}\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}dt,

and

\displaystyle B_{I}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\||x|^{-1}*|u_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}+\||x|^{-1}*|v_{hi}|^{2}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{5}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{5}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times[\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{19}{4}}_{L^{\frac{19}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{114}{7}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{19}{4}}_{L^{\frac{19}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{114}{7}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t,x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{9}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{9}{4}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}][\|\nabla u_{hi}\|^{\frac{19}{4}}_{L^{\frac{19}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{38}{15}}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{hi}\|^{\frac{19}{4}}_{L^{\frac{19}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{38}{15}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}+N^{-3}\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}dt.

By the choosing of the parameters, the terms containing
B_{I}
and
[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]
on the righthand side can be absorbed by the left ones, we get

\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad[\|u_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{4}_{L^{4}_{t,x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta_{0}(N^{-3}+K).\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\hfill\Box

5.6
Impossibility of quasi-solution

Proposition 5.38(No quasi-solutions).

There are no almost periodic solutions
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
of (
1.6
) with
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on each characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})

which satisfy

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v\|_{L^{10}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}=+\infty,\quad\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}(t)dt=+\infty.

(5.172)

Proof:
Contradictorily, assume that such a solution
(u,v)
exists.

Since
(u(t,x),v(t,x))
is not identically zero for each times
t
and
(u,v)
is almost periodic, there exists
C(u,v)>0
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{N}(t)\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u(t,x)|^{4}+|v(t,x)|^{4}]dx\geq\frac{1}{C(u,v)}

(5.173)

uniformly for
t\in[0,T_{\max})
. Using Hölder’s inequality, we have

\widetilde{N}(t)\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u_{\leq N}(t,x)|^{4}+|v_{\leq N}(t,x)|^{4}]dx\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|u_{\leq N}(t,\cdot)\|^{4}_{L^{6}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}(t,\cdot)\|^{4}_{L^{6}_{x}}]

for any
N>0
uniformly for
t\in[0,T_{\max})
. Recalling the results of Proposition 5.30 and (
5.173
), we know that for each
\eta_{0}>0
, there exists some
N=N(\eta_{0})
sufficiently small such that

\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta_{0}N^{-3}+\eta_{0}\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt

uniformly for time intervals
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
that are a union of characteristic subintervals
J_{k}
. Particularly, we can chose
\eta_{0}
small enough such that the term on the righthand side containing
\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt
can be absorbed by the one on the left side. Consequently,

\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt=\lim_{T\uparrow T_{\max}}\int_{0}^{T}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt\lesssim_{(u,v)}1,

which is a contradiction to (
5.172
).
\Box

Now we have precluded the existence of the two types of almost periodic solution described in Proposition 5.6 and proved Theorem 6.

6
\dot{H}^{1}\times\dot{H}^{1}
scattering theories for the global solution of (
1.6
) with defocusing nonlinearities when
d=4

In this section, we will establish
\dot{H}^{1}\times\dot{H}^{1}
scattering theories for the global solution of (
1.6
) in defocusing case with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
when
d=4
.

The organization of this section is similar to that of Section 5. We begin with the following definition, some propositions and lemmas below. The proofs of Proposition 6.2, Lemma 6.3 and Corollary 6.4 had been proved in Section 5 as well as those of Proposition 5.2, Lemma 5.3 and Corollary 5.4. The proofs of Lemma 6.5 and Proposition 6.7 are respectively similar to those of Lemma 5.5 and Proposition 5.7, we omit the details here.

Definition 6.1(Almost periodicity).

A solution
(u,v)\in[L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})]^{2}
of (
1.6
) is said to be almost periodic(modulo symmetries) if there exist functions
\widetilde{N}:I\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}
,
\tilde{x}:I\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{4}
, and
C:\mathbb{R}^{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}

such that for all
t\in I
and
\eta>0

\displaystyle\int_{|x-\tilde{x}(t)|\geq\frac{C(\eta)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|\nabla u(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v(t,x)|^{2}]dx+\int_{|\xi|\geq C(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)}|\xi|^{2}[|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi\leq\eta.

(6.1)

The functions
\widetilde{N}(t)
,
\tilde{x}(t)
and
C(\eta)
are called as the frequency scale function for the solution
(u,v)
, the spatial center function and the modulus of compactness respectively.

By compactness, there exists
c(\eta)>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{|x-\tilde{x}(t)|\leq\frac{c(\eta)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|\nabla u(t,x)|^{2}+|\nabla v(t,x)|^{2}]dx+\int_{|\xi|\leq c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)}|\xi|^{2}[|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi\leq\eta.

(6.2)

Proposition 6.2(Reduction to almost periodic solution).

Assume that Theorem 7 failed. Then there exists a maximal-lifespan solution
(u,v):I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}

of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
which is almost periodic and blows up both forward and backward in time in the sense that for all
t_{0}\in I
,

\int_{t_{0}}^{\sup I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}[|u(t,x)|^{6}+|v(t,x)|^{6}]dxdt=\int^{t_{0}}_{\inf I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}[|u(t,x)|^{6}+|v(t,x)|^{6}]dxdt=+\infty.

Lemma 6.3(Local constancy property).

Let
(u,v):I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
be a maximal-lifespan almost period solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
. Then there exists a small number
\delta
which depends only on
(u,v)
, such that

[t_{0}-\delta\widetilde{N}(t_{0})^{-2},t_{0}+\delta\widetilde{N}(t_{0})^{-2}]\subset I\quad{\rm if}\quad t_{0}\in I,

and

\widetilde{N}(t)\thicksim_{(u,v)}\widetilde{N}(t_{0})\quad{\rm whenever}\quad|t-t_{0}|\leq\delta\widetilde{N}(t_{0})^{-2}.

Corollary 6.4(
\widetilde{N}(t)
blows up).

Let
(u,v):I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
be a maximal-lifespan almost period solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
. If
T
is any finite endpoint of
I
, then
\widetilde{N}(t)\geq_{(u,v)}|T-t|^{-\frac{1}{2}}
. Consequently,
\lim_{t\rightarrow T}\tilde{N}(t)=+\infty
.

Lemma 6.5(Spacetime bounds).

Let
(u,v)
be an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
on a time interval
I
. Then for any admissible pair
(q,r)
with
2\leq q<+\infty
,

\displaystyle\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}dt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla u\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}dt

(6.3)

Since it can be realized by a simple rescaling argument, we can assume that
\widetilde{N}(t)\geq 1
at least on half of
I
, say, on
[0,T_{\max})
. Finally, we get

Proposition 6.6(Two special scenarios for blowup).

Assume that Theorem 7 failed. Then there exists an almost periodic solution
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
such that

\|u\|_{L_{t,x}^{6}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v\|_{L_{t,x}^{6}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}=+\infty

and
[0,T_{\max})=\cup_{k}J_{k}
, where
J_{k}
are characteristic intervals on which
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
.
Moreover,

{\rm either}\quad\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty\quad{\rm or}\quad\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt=+\infty.

Similarly, we only need to preclude the existence of the two types of almost periodic solution described in Proposition 6.6, then we can give the proof of Theorem 7. To prove the no-existence of cascade solutions, we also need the following proposition.

Proposition 6.7(No-waste Duhamel formulae).

Let
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
be defined as in Proposition 6.6. Then for all
t\in[0,T_{\max})

\displaystyle u(t)=i\lim_{T\rightarrow T_{\max}}\int_{t}^{T}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}|v|^{2}u(s)ds,\quad v(t)=i\lim_{T\rightarrow T_{\max}}\int_{t}^{T}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}|u|^{2}v(s)ds

(6.4)

in the weak
\dot{H}^{1}_{x}
topology.

6.1
Long-time Strichartz estimates

In this subsection, we give the Long-time Strichartz estimates.

The following lemmas are a consequence of dispersive estimates for the free Schrödinger propagator
e^{it\Delta}
, which was proved in
[
36
,
42
,
79
]
.

Lemma 6.8(Strichartz inequality).

Assume that
I
is a compact time interval and
w:I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
is a solution of the forced Schödinger equation

iw_{t}+\Delta w=G\quad{\rm for\ some\ function}\quad G.

Then for any time
t_{0}\in I
and admissible pairs
(q,r)
and
(\tilde{q},\tilde{r})
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q}+\frac{2}{r}=\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}+\frac{2}{\tilde{r}}=1\quad{\rm and}\quad 2\leq q,\tilde{q}\leq\infty,

the following inequality holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla w\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim\|w(t_{0})\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla G\|_{L^{\tilde{q}^{\prime}}_{t}L^{\tilde{r}^{\prime}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})},

where
\tilde{q}^{\prime}
is the dual exponent to
\tilde{q}
and satisfies
\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{q}^{\prime}}=1
.

The following Lemmas can be found in
[
7
,
21
,
72
,
93
,
94
]
.

Lemma 6.9(Bilinear Strichartz).

For any spacetime slab
I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
and any frequencies
M>0
and
N>0
,

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq M}v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim\frac{M^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\nabla u_{\leq M}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(I)}\|v_{\geq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(I)}.

Here the norm
\|\cdot\|_{S^{*}_{0}(I)}
is defined as

\|u\|_{S^{*}_{0}(I)}:=\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|(i\partial_{t}+\Delta)u\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}.

Lemma 6.10(Paraproduct estimate).

\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(fg)\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}f\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}g\|_{L^{q}_{x}}

for any
1<p,q<+\infty
satisfying
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=\frac{11}{12}
.

Inspired by
[
22
,
94
]
, we give the long-time Strichartz estimates below.

Proposition 6.11(Long-time Strichartz estimates).

Let
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}

be an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) with
(\alpha,\beta)=(0,0)
and
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on each characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
. Then
for any frequency
N>0
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad{\rm with}\quad K:=\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt

(6.5)

is true on any compact time interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous intervals
J_{k}
. And for any
\eta>0
there exists
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)>0
such that for all
N\leq N_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta[1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}].

(6.6)

Here the constant
N_{0}
and the implicit constants in (
6.5
) and (
6.6
) are independent of the interval
I
.

Proof:
Let
I
be a fixed compact time interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous intervals
J_{k}
. Unless specifying, we let all spacetime norms be on
I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
throughout the proof. Let
\eta_{0}
be a small parameter to be chosen later. By the definition of almost periodicity symmtries, for this
\eta_{0}
, there exists
c_{0}=c_{0}(\eta_{0})
such that

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\leq\eta_{0}.

(6.7)

For
N>0
, we define

A(N):=\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}.

By the result of Lemma 6.5,

\displaystyle A(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}\quad{\rm for\ any}\quad N\geq\left(\frac{\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}dt}{\int_{I}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt}\right)^{\frac{1}{3}},

(6.8)

especially for
N\geq N_{\max}:=\sup_{t\in I}\widetilde{N}(t)
.

For arbitrary frequencies
N>0
, we will establish the result by induction. First, we will establish a recurrence relation for
A(N)
. Using Strichartz’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle A(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\inf_{t\in I}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}.

(6.9)

We only give the details of estimating
\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}
below because the estimating
\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}
is entirely similar. To do this, we decompose
u=u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}+u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}
and
v=v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}+v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}
, then further decompose

u=u_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}+u_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}
and
v=v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}+v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}
and write

\displaystyle\nabla f_{1}(u,v)

\displaystyle=\varnothing[\nabla(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})]+\varnothing[\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]+\varnothing[\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing[\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing[\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]+\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}u_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing[\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}u_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]+\varnothing[\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}u_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing[\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}u_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]

\displaystyle:=(I1)+(I2)+(I3)+(I4)+(I5)+(I6)+(I7)

\displaystyle\quad+(II1)+(II2)+(II3)+(II4)+(II5)+(II6)+(II7)+(II8).

(6.10)

We will estimate the contributions of each of these terms to (
6.10
).

Using Berstein inequality, Hölder’s inequality and Sobolev embedding, the contribution of (I1) can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\quad\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})]\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim N^{\frac{5}{3}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{5}{3}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}(v^{2}_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{5}{3}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}(v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}\|v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{5}{3}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M).

(6.11)

The contributions of (I2), (II1) and (II2) can be obtained similarly as follows:

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M),

(6.12)

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M),

(6.13)

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}]\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M),

(6.14)

The contribution of (I3) can be estimated as follows:
