\displaystyle\quad\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)})]\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim N^{\frac{2}{3}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{2}{3}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}(\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{2}{3}}\||v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}\||\nabla|^{\frac{1}{3}}(u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{2}{3}}A(\frac{N}{\eta_{0}})\eta_{0}(\frac{N}{\eta_{0}})^{-\frac{2}{3}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta_{0}^{\frac{5}{3}}A(\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}).

(6.15)

The contribution of (I4) is

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla u_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{J_{k}\subset I}\|\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}.

(6.16)

The contribution of (I5) is

\displaystyle\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}_{0}A(\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}).

(6.17)

In convenience, we divide the time interval
I
into subintervals
J_{k}
where
\widetilde{N}(t)
is constant and use the bilinear Strichartz estimate in Lemma 6.9 on each of these subintervals. The contribution of (I6) is

\displaystyle\quad\|P_{\leq N}\varnothing[\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v^{2}_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}]\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}\lesssim\|v_{>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|\|\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}\|^{2}_{L_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{J_{k}\subset I}\|\Delta u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{J_{k}\subset I}\|\Delta u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}.

(6.18)

The contribution of (I7) can be controlled by the sum of those of (I5) and (I6) because

|v_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}v_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}|\leq|v^{2}_{>c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}|+|v^{2}_{\leq c_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)}|.

The contributions of (II3), (II4), (II5) and (II6) are similar to those of (I3), (I4), (I5) and (I6) respectively, while the contributions of (II7) and
(II8) can be controlled by the sum of those of (II5) and (II6). Therefore, we can put all of the results above together and get the following recurrence relation

\displaystyle A(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\inf_{t\in I}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{J_{k}\subset I}[\|\nabla u_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}].

(6.19)

First, we address (
6.5
). Note that (
6.8
) holds for
N\geq N_{\max}
. That is, there exists some constant
C(u,v)>0
such that

\displaystyle A(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}C(u,v)[1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}]\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad N\geq N_{\max}.

(6.20)

Rewriting (
6.19
) as

\displaystyle A(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\tilde{C}(u,v)\left\{1+\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M)\right\},

(6.21)

through halving the frequency
N
at each step, we can prove (
6.5
) inductively. Without loss of generality, we assume that (
6.20
)
holds for frequencies larger or equal to
N
, and apply (
6.21
) with
\eta_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}
such that

\displaystyle A(\frac{N}{2})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}(u,v)\left\{1+\frac{(\frac{N}{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{2\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{2M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}(u,v)\left\{1+\frac{(\frac{N}{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{2\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{2M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M)\right\}.

If we chose
\eta_{0}=\eta_{0}(u,v)
small enough to satisfies
\eta_{0}^{\frac{1}{6}}\tilde{C}(u,v)\leq\frac{1}{2}
, then

\displaystyle A(\frac{N}{2})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}C(u,v)\left\{1+(\frac{N}{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}+\tilde{C}(u,v)\left\{1+\frac{(\frac{N}{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right\}.

Taking
C(u,v)\geq 2\tilde{C}(u,v)\eta_{0}^{-\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{-\frac{3}{2}}
, we can get (
6.5
).

Now we turn to (
6.6
). To show the small constant
\eta
, we will prove the following lemma.

Lemma 6.12(Vanishing of the small frequencies).

Under the assumptions of Proposition 6.11, there holds

\displaystyle f(N)

\displaystyle:=\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}]\rightarrow 0\quad{\rm as}\quad N\rightarrow 0.

Proof:
Under the assumption of Proposition 6.11, by compactness, we obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow 0}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}]=0.

(6.22)

Fixing a characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
, by the spacetime bounds in Lemma 6.5, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|u\|_{L^{3}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}+\|u\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{3}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1.

All spacetime norms in the estimates above are on
J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
. For any frequency
N
, decomposing
u=u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}+u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}
and
v=v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}+v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, we get

\displaystyle\quad\ \|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}

\displaystyle=\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u,v)\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}},v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v^{2}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}vu\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}},v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}+\|\varnothing(\nabla v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}u^{2}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varnothing(\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}vu\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle:=\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+(I1)+(I2)+(I3)+(I4)+(I5)+(I6)+(I7)

\displaystyle\qquad+(II1)+(II2)+(II3)+(II4)+(II5)+(II6)+(II7).

Using Hölder’s inequality and Bernstein’s inequality,

\displaystyle(I1)

\displaystyle\lesssim N\|u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}}\|v_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle(I2)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla u_{>N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle(I6)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v\|^{2}_{L^{3}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}.

The estimate for (II1) is similar to that of (I1). The estimates for (I3), (I4), (I5), (II2), (II3), (II4) and (II5) are similar to that of (I2), they are all bounded by
N^{\frac{1}{2}}
. The estimate for (II6) is similar to that of (I6). While (I7) and (II7) can be controlled by the sum of (I6) and (II6) because

|uv|\leq|u|^{2}+|v|^{2}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\quad\ \|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+N^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

All spacetime norms in the estimates above are also on
J_{k}\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
. Since
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
is arbitrary, we get

\displaystyle\sup_{J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{S^{*}_{0}(J_{k})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla u_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}.

This combines (
6.22
) prove the claim of this lemma.
\Box

Now we come back to the proof of (
6.6
). Using (
6.5
) and Lemma 6.12 , (
6.19
) means that

\displaystyle A(N)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}f(N)+\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}f(N)+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A(M)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}f(N)+\eta^{\frac{5}{3}}_{0}+\left(\frac{f(N)}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\eta^{\frac{1}{6}}_{0}\right)N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Hence, for any
\eta>0
, first we can chose
\eta_{0}=\eta_{0}(\eta)
satisfying
\eta^{\frac{1}{6}}_{0}\leq\eta
, then
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)
such that

\frac{f(N)}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\eta
, and obtain

A(N)\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+N^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}})\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad N\leq N_{0}.

Proposition 6.12 is proved.
\Box

Corollary 6.13(Low and high frequencies control).

Let
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}

be an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) with
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on each characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
. Then
for any frequency
N>0
and the admissible pair
(q,r)

\displaystyle\|u_{\geq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}\left(1+N^{3}K\right)^{\frac{1}{q}},\quad 3<q\leq+\infty

(6.23)

is true on any compact time interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous intervals
J_{k}
. And for any
\eta>0
there exists
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)>0
such that for all
N\leq N_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta\left(1+N^{3}K\right)^{\frac{1}{q}},\quad 2\leq q\leq+\infty.

(6.24)

Here the constant
N_{0}
and the implicit constants in (
6.23
) and (
6.24
) are independent of the interval
I
.

Proof:
First, we address (
6.23
). Using (
6.5
) and Berstein’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}u_{\geq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{M\geq N}M^{-\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}[\|\nabla u_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{M\geq N}M^{-\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}(1+M^{\frac{3}{2}}K^{\frac{1}{2}})\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-\frac{3}{2}-\epsilon}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{2}}

for any
\epsilon>0
and any frequencies
N>0
. Interpolating with the energy bound, we have

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{\geq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v_{\geq N}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{q-3}{2}}u_{\geq N}\|^{\frac{2}{q}}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\|\nabla u_{\geq N}\|^{1-\frac{2}{q}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{q-3}{2}}v_{\geq N}\|^{\frac{2}{q}}_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\|\nabla v_{\geq N}\|^{1-\frac{2}{q}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{q}},

where
(q,r)
is admissible pair with
3<q\leq+\infty
.

Now it is the turn of (
6.24
). Since
\inf_{t\in I}\widetilde{N}(t)\geq 1
, by Remark, for any
\eta>0
, there exists
N_{0}(\eta)
such that for all
N\leq N_{0}
,

\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\leq\eta.

Interpolating with (
6.6
), we obtain the claim.
\Box

6.2
The rapid frequency-cascade scenario

In this subsection, we preclude the existence of almost periodic solutions as in Proposition 6.6 for which
\int^{T_{\max}}_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty
.

Proposition 6.14(No rapid frequency-cascades).

There are no almost periodic solutions
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
with
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on each characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
satisfying
\|u\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}=+\infty
and

\displaystyle\int^{T_{\max}}_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt<+\infty.

(6.25)

Proof:
Contradictorily, assume that
(u,v)
is such a solution. By Corollary 6.4, whether
T_{\max}
is finite or infinite,

\displaystyle\lim_{t\rightarrow T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)=+\infty.

(6.26)

By compactness, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{t\rightarrow T_{\max}}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]=0\quad{\rm for\ any}\quad N>0.

(6.27)

Let
I_{n}
be a nested sequence of compact subintervals of
[0,T_{\max})
which are unions of contiguous characteristic subintervals
J_{k}
. Applying Proposition 6.11 on each
I_{n}
, noticing (
6.19
) and (
6.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle A_{n}(N)

\displaystyle:=\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I_{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I_{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\inf_{t\in I}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A_{n}(M)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}[\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-1}dt\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\inf_{t\in I}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]+\sum_{M>\frac{N}{\eta_{0}}}\left(\frac{N}{M}\right)^{\frac{5}{3}}A_{n}(M)+\frac{N^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\eta_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}

for all frequencies
N>0
. Similar to the proof of (
6.5
), we get, for all
N>0
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I_{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}(I_{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\inf_{t\in I_{n}}[\|\nabla u_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}]+N^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Letting
n\rightarrow+\infty
and recalling (
6.27
), we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(6.28)

Next, we hope to show that (
6.28
) implies, for all
N>0
,

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(6.29)

Decomposing
u=u_{\leq N}+u_{>N}
and
v=v_{\leq N}+v_{>N}
, by the no-waste Duhamel formulae in Proposition 6.9, using Strichartz’s inequality, Bernstein’s inequality, Hölder’s inequality and Sobolev embedding, we get

\displaystyle\quad\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{1}(u_{\leq N},v_{\leq N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v_{\leq N}v_{>N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(u_{\leq N}v_{>N}v_{>N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}u_{>N}v^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}f_{2}(u_{\leq N},v_{\leq N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(v_{\leq N}u_{\leq N}u_{>N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla P_{\leq N}\varnothing(v_{\leq N}u_{>N}u_{>N})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\nabla v\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}][\|u_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|u_{>N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|v_{>N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+N^{\frac{5}{3}}[\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}u_{>N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{2}{3}}v_{>N}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}][\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{2}{3}}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{12}{5}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times[\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{M>N}M^{-\frac{5}{3}}[\|\nabla u_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{M}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}+N^{\frac{5}{3}}N^{-\frac{1}{6}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Here all spacetime norms in the estimates above are on
[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
. (
6.29
) is proved.

Now we are ready to finish the proof of this proposition. Using Bernstein inequality and (
6.29
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}u_{>1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}u_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}v_{>1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{-\frac{1}{4}}v_{\leq 1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\sum_{N>1}N^{-\frac{5}{4}}+\sum_{N\leq 1}N^{\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}1,

which means
u,v\in L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{-\frac{1}{4}}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})
.

Fix
t\in[0,T_{\max})
and let
\eta>0
be a small constant. By compactness, there exists
c(\eta)>0
such that

\int_{|\xi|\leq c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)}|\xi|^{2}|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}d\xi+\int_{|\xi|\leq c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)}|\xi|^{2}|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}d\xi\leq\eta.

Interpolating with
u\in L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{-\frac{1}{4}}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})
and
v\in L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{-\frac{1}{4}}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|\xi|\leq c(\eta)\hat{N}(t)}[|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{5}}.

(6.30)

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{|\xi|\geq c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)}[|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi\leq[c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}|\xi|^{2}[|\hat{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}[c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-2}.

(6.31)

Using Plancherel’s theorem, (
6.30
) and (
6.31
), we get

0\leq M(u,v)(t):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}[|u(t,x)|^{2}+|v(t,x)|^{2}]dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}[|\tilde{u}(t,\xi)|^{2}+|\hat{v}(t,\xi)|^{2}]d\xi\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{\frac{1}{5}}+[c(\eta)\widetilde{N}(t)]^{-2}

for all
t\in[0,T_{\max})
. Recalling (
6.26
) and letting
\eta\rightarrow 0
, we know that
M(u,v)\equiv 0
, which implies that
(u,v)\equiv 0
.
It is a contradiction to
\|u\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}=+\infty
. Proposition 6.14 is proved.
\Box

6.3
The quasi-solution scenario

In this subsection, we preclude the existence of almost periodic solutions as in Proposition 6.6 for which
\int^{T_{\max}}_{0}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt=+\infty
.
If there exists such a solution, we will use the weight-coupled interaction Morawetz inequality to deduce a contradiction.

Assume that
(\phi,\psi)
is a solution of

\displaystyle i\phi_{t}+\Delta\phi=\lambda|\psi|^{2}\phi+\mathcal{F}_{1},\quad i\psi_{t}+\Delta\psi=\mu|\phi|^{2}\psi+\mathcal{F}_{2}

(6.32)

when the spatial dimension
d=4
. Similar to (
5.116
) in the case of the spatial dimension
d=3
, for some weight
a:\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, we also define the following weight-coupled Morawetz interaction:

\displaystyle M^{\otimes_{2}}_{a}(t)

\displaystyle=2A\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,x)\bar{\phi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\phi(t,x)\phi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2B\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\psi}(t,x)\bar{\psi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\psi(t,x)\psi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\phi}(t,x)\bar{\psi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\phi(t,x)\psi(t,y))]dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+2D\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\widetilde{\nabla}a(x-y)\Im[\bar{\psi}(t,x)\bar{\phi}(t,y)\widetilde{\nabla}(\psi(t,x)\phi(t,y))]dxdy,

(6.33)

where
\widetilde{\nabla}=(\nabla_{x},\nabla_{y})
,
x\in\mathbb{R}^{4}
and
y\in\mathbb{R}^{4}
,
A=4\mu^{2}
,
B=4\lambda^{2}
and
C=D=4\lambda\mu
. If we take
a(x,y)=|x-y|
in the weight-coupled interaction Morawetz action (
6.33
), then

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}M(t)^{\otimes}_{2}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|\phi(t,x)|^{2}+|\psi(t,x)|^{2}][|\phi(t,y)|^{2}+|\psi(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdy

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{x}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{x}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m})\cdot\frac{x-y}{|x-y|}dx|w_{n}(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{y}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{y}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m})\cdot\frac{y-x}{|x-y|}dy|w_{n}(t,x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{x-y}{|x-y|}\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,x)\nabla_{x}w_{m}(t,x)]dx\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,y)dy

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{y-x}{|x-y|}\cdot\Im[\bar{w}_{m}(t,y)\nabla_{y}w_{m}(t,y)]dy\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,x)dx.

(6.34)

Here
w_{1}
,
w_{2}
can be taken as follows: (1) one is
\phi
, another is
\psi
; (2) both are
\phi
; (3) both are
\psi
.
Denote

\displaystyle\{\mathcal{F}_{m},\phi,\psi\}_{px}:=\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{x}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{x}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}),\quad\{\mathcal{F}_{m},\phi,\psi\}_{py}:=\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{y}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{y}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m}),

(6.35)

\displaystyle\{\mathcal{F}_{n},\phi,\psi\}_{ix}:=\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,x),\quad\{\mathcal{F}_{n},\phi,\psi\}_{iy}:=\Im(\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{n}w_{n})(t,y).

(6.36)

Integrating (
6.34
) with respect to time, we get

Proposition 6.15(Weight-coupled interaction Morawetz inequality).

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|\phi(t,x)|^{2}+|\psi(t,x)|^{2}][|\phi(t,y)|^{2}+|\psi(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle+\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\{\mathcal{F}_{m},\phi,\psi\}_{px}\cdot\frac{x-y}{|x-y|}dx|w_{n}(t,y)|^{2}dydt

\displaystyle+\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\{\mathcal{F}_{m},\phi,\psi\}_{py}\cdot\frac{y-x}{|x-y|}dy|w_{n}(t,x)|^{2}dxdt

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\phi\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\psi\|^{3}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}][\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}+\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}]+\left\{[\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.[\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}+\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}][\|\{\mathcal{F}_{1},\phi,\psi\}_{ix}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|\{\mathcal{F}_{2},\phi,\psi\}_{ix}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}]\right\}.

(6.37)

All spacetime norms above are on
I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
.

Letting
\phi=u_{\geq N}
,
\psi=v_{\geq N}
,
\mathcal{F}_{1}=P_{\geq N}(|v|^{2}u)
and
\mathcal{F}_{2}=P_{\geq N}(|u|^{2}v)
for
N
small enough such that
the Littlewood-Paley projection captures most of the solution, we will use Proposition 6.15 to prove

Proposition 6.16(Frequency-localized interaction Morawetz estimate).

Let
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}\times\mathbb{C}
be an almost periodic solution of (
1.6
) satisfying
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on every characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
. Then for any
\eta>0
there exists
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)
such
that

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta[N^{-3}+\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt]

(6.38)

for
N\leq N_{0}
and any compact time interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous subintervals
J_{k}
. Here the implicit constant does not depend on the interval
I
.

Proof:
Let
I
be a fixed compact interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous subintervals
J_{k}
, denote
K:=\int_{I}N(t)^{-1}dt
.
All spacetime norms will be on
I\times\mathbb{R}^{4}
in the proof of this proposition below.

Fix
\eta>0
and chose
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)
small enough such that the claims in Corollary 6.13 hold for all
N\leq N_{0}
. Especially, fixing
N\leq N_{0}
and writing
u_{lo}:=u_{\leq N}
,
v_{lo}:=v_{\leq N}
,
u_{hi}:=u_{>N}
and
v_{hi}:=v_{>N}
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{q}},\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad\frac{1}{q}+\frac{2}{r}=1,\quad 2\leq q\leq+\infty,

(6.39)

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{q}_{t}L^{r}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{q}},\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad\frac{1}{q}+\frac{2}{r}=1,\quad 3<q\leq+\infty,

(6.40)

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{6}N^{-1}.

(6.41)

Applying Proposition 6.15 with
\phi=u_{hi}
,
\psi=v_{hi}
,
\mathcal{F}_{1}=P_{hi}f_{1}(u,v)
and
\mathcal{F}_{2}=P_{hi}f_{2}(u,v)
, and using (
6.41
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+2\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{x}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{x}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m})\cdot\frac{x-y}{|x-y|}dx|w_{n}(t,y)|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\quad+2\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{y}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{y}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m})\cdot\frac{y-x}{|x-y|}dy|w_{n}(t,x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{18}N^{-3}+\eta^{6}N^{-1}[\|\{\mathcal{F}_{1},u_{hi},v_{hi}\}_{ix}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|\{\mathcal{F}_{2},u_{hi},v_{hi}\}_{ix}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}].

(6.42)

Here
w_{1}
,
w_{2}
can be taken as follows: (1) one is
u_{hi}
, another is
v_{hi}
; (2) both are
u_{hi}
; (3) both are
v_{hi}
.

Since

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{m,n=1}^{2}\Re(\mathcal{F}_{m}\nabla_{x}\bar{w}_{m}-w_{m}\nabla_{x}\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{m})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}[\varnothing(u_{hi}^{2}v_{hi}^{2})+\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo})]

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla_{x}[\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}u_{lo}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}v_{lo}u^{2}_{lo})]

\displaystyle\quad+[\varnothing(v_{hi}^{2}u_{hi})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})]\nabla_{x}u_{lo}

\displaystyle\quad+[\varnothing(u_{hi}^{2}v_{hi})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u^{2}_{lo})]\nabla_{x}v_{lo}

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla_{x}[\varnothing(u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v))+\varnothing(v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v))]+\varnothing(u_{hi}\nabla_{x}P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v))+\varnothing(v_{hi}\nabla_{x}P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v))

and

\displaystyle\{\mathcal{F}_{1},u_{hi},v_{hi}\}_{ix}

\displaystyle=\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo})

\displaystyle\{\mathcal{F}_{2},u_{hi},v_{hi}\}_{ix}

\displaystyle=\varnothing(v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}),

using (
6.39
)–(
6.41
), we obtain from (
6.42
)

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{18}N^{-3}+\eta^{6}N^{-1}I(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})+\eta^{12}N^{-2}II(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}]J(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}][P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)||u_{hi}|+|P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v)||v_{hi}|]}{|x-y|}dxdydt,

(6.43)

where

\displaystyle I(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})

\displaystyle=\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|v^{2}_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|u_{hi}P_{hi}f_{1}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}P_{hi}f_{2}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}(u_{hi},v_{hi})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2}(u_{hi},v_{hi})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}},

(6.44)

\displaystyle II(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})

\displaystyle=\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}u_{hi}u_{lo}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}+\|v_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}},

(6.45)

\displaystyle J(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})

\displaystyle=\varnothing(u_{hi}u_{lo}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}v_{lo}u^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u^{2}_{lo})

\displaystyle\quad+\varnothing(u^{2}_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}).

(6.46)

We estimate the terms through (
6.44
)–(
6.46
) below.

Using (
6.39
)–(
6.41
) and Sobolev embedding, we obtain

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{7}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{14}{5}}_{x}}\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{7}{3}}_{t}L^{28}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K),\quad\|v^{2}_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)\quad{\rm because}\ |u_{hi}v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}|\leq|u^{2}_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|+|v^{2}_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}|.

Using Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}P_{hi}f_{1}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}N^{-1}\|\nabla f_{1}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{5}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{4}}[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{16}{3}}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{16}{3}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{3}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)

and

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}P_{lo}f_{1}(u_{hi},v_{hi})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}N^{\frac{7}{5}}\|f_{1}(u_{hi},v_{hi})\|_{L^{\frac{10}{7}}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{2}{5}}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{3}{10}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{7}{3}}_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{7}{3}}_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}][\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{40}{11}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{40}{11}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{\frac{2}{5}-\frac{7}{3}}(1+N^{3}K)[\||\nabla|^{\frac{9}{10}}u_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{\frac{9}{10}}v_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|v_{hi}P_{hi}f_{2}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{3}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|v_{hi}P_{lo}f_{2}(u_{hi},v_{hi})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K).

(
6.44
) is completed.

Consider the terms in (
6.45
). Using (
6.39
)–(
6.41
), Sobolev embedding and Bernstein inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{7}{3}}_{t}L^{28}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{7}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{4}{5}}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}]\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{9}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{hi}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),\quad\|v_{hi}u_{hi}u_{lo}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\|v_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{9}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K).

Write

f_{1}(u,v)=f_{1}(u_{lo},v_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}).

We estimate

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}f_{1}(u_{lo},v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}[\|\nabla u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{9}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}N^{\frac{12}{5}}\|u_{hi}v^{2}_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{7}}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{12}{5}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}[\|u^{3}_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{7}}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}+\|v^{3}_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{7}}_{t}L^{1}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim N^{\frac{12}{5}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{7}{3}}_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{40}{11}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{7}{3}}_{L^{\frac{10}{3}}_{t}L^{\frac{20}{7}}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|^{\frac{2}{3}}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{40}{11}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim N\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{7}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{14}{5}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{7}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{14}{5}}_{x}}]\|v_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{7}{3}}_{t}L^{28}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim N\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}[\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim N[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}][\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim N\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{7}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{14}{5}}_{x}}\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{7}{3}}_{t}L^{28}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K),

\displaystyle\quad\|u_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}u^{2}_{lo})\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}\lesssim N\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|u_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}N^{-1}(1+N^{3}K).

Similarly, we can obtain the estimate for
\|v_{hi}\nabla P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v)\|_{L^{1}_{t,x}}
. (
6.45
) is completed.

Now we consider the contribution of the term containing
J(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})
. We estimate

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)||u_{lo}(t,x)||v_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}]\|\frac{1}{|x|}*(|u_{hi}||u_{lo}||v_{lo}|^{2})\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}[\|u_{hi}u^{3}_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t,x}}+\|u_{hi}v^{3}_{lo}\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t,x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}[\|u_{lo}\|^{3}_{L^{\frac{18}{5}}_{t}L^{9}_{x}}+\|v_{lo}\|^{3}_{L^{\frac{18}{5}}_{t}L^{9}_{x}}]\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{9}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|v_{hi}(t,x)||v_{lo}(t,x)||u_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{9}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K).

Note that
|u^{2}_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo}|\lesssim 10^{-6}|u_{hi}|^{2}|v_{hi}|^{2}+|u_{hi}|^{2}|v_{lo}|^{2}
and

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)||v_{lo}(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim 10^{-6}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt.

Using Hölder’s inequality and Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality, we have

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}]\|\frac{1}{|x|}*(|u_{hi}||u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}\||u_{hi}|(|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}\||u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|(|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{5}{12}}\|(|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{5}{12}}N\|(|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{17}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{5}{12}}N\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{5}{12}}\eta^{2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(N^{-3}+K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|u_{hi}(t,x)||u_{lo}(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim 10^{-6}\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|u_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt.

and

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|v_{hi}(t,x)|^{2}|u_{lo}(t,x)|^{2}}{|x-y|}dxdydt\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(N^{-3}+K),

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)||v_{hi}(t,x)||u_{lo}(t,x)||v_{lo}(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(N^{-3}+K)\quad{\rm because}\quad|u_{hi}||v_{hi}||u_{lo}||v_{lo}|\lesssim|u_{hi}|^{2}|v_{lo}|^{2}+|v_{hi}|^{2}|u_{lo}|^{2}.

The contribution of the term containing
J(u_{hi},v_{hi},u_{lo},v_{lo})
is completed.

Last, we estimate the terms containing
P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)
and
P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v)
respectively. Write

f_{1}(u,v)=f_{1}(u_{hi},v_{hi})+\varnothing(u_{lo}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})+\varnothing(v^{2}_{hi}u_{lo})+\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})+\varnothing(v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}).

Using Hölder’s inequality and Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)||P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|u_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}+\|v_{hi}\|^{2}_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}]\|\frac{1}{|x|}*(|u_{hi}|||P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)(t,x)||)\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}\||u_{hi}|(|u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|)\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{1}{6}}\||u_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|(||P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)(t,x)||)\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{5}{12}}\|(||P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)(t,x)||)\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}.

While

\displaystyle\quad\|P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{lo}v^{2}_{lo})\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\lesssim\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{5}}_{x}}\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{3}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}},

\displaystyle\quad\|P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v^{2}_{lo})\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\lesssim N\|v^{2}_{lo}u_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{17}}_{x}}\lesssim\|u_{hi}\|_{L^{12}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\|v_{lo}\|^{2}_{L^{4}_{t}L^{8}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}},

\displaystyle\quad\|P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{lo}v^{2}_{hi})\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\lesssim N\|u_{lo}v^{2}_{hi}\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{17}}_{x}}\lesssim_{u,v}N\|u_{lo}\|_{L^{3}_{t}L^{12}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|v_{hi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}}

and similarly

\displaystyle\|P_{lo}\varnothing(u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo})\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}},\quad\|P_{lo}\varnothing(v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo})\|_{L^{\frac{12}{7}}_{t}L^{\frac{24}{11}}_{x}}\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta(1+N^{3}K)^{\frac{7}{12}}

because
|u_{hi}v_{hi}v_{lo}|\leq[|u^{2}_{hi}v_{lo}|+|v^{2}_{hi}v_{lo}|
and
|v_{hi}u_{lo}v_{lo}|\leq|v_{hi}u^{2}_{lo}|+|v_{hi}v^{2}_{lo}|
.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)||P_{lo}f_{1}(u,v)(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt\lesssim\eta(N^{-3}+K).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{hi}(t,y)|^{2}]|u_{hi}(t,x)||P_{lo}f_{2}(u,v)(t,x)|}{|x-y|}dxdydt\lesssim\eta(N^{-3}+K).

Putting all things above together, we obtain (
6.38
).
\Box

Now we are ready to preclude the second scenario of Proposition 6.6 and complete the proof of Theorem 7.

Proposition 6.17(No quasi-solitions).

There are no almost periodic solution
(u,v):[0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
satisfying
\widetilde{N}(t)\equiv N_{k}\geq 1
on each characteristic interval
J_{k}\subset[0,T_{\max})
and

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v\|_{L^{6}_{t,x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}=+\infty,\quad\int_{0}^{T_{\max}}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt=+\infty.

(6.47)

Proof:
Contradictorily, assume that there exists such a solution
(u,v)
.

Let
\eta>0
be a small parameter which will be chosen later. By the results of Proposition 6.16, we can find some
N_{0}=N_{0}(\eta)
such that for all
N\leq N_{0}
and any compact time interval
I\subset[0,T_{\max})
, which is a union of contiguous subintervals
J_{k}
,

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta[N^{-3}+\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt].

(6.48)

Noticing that
\inf_{t\in[0,T_{\max})}\widetilde{N}(t)\geq 1
, we can chose
N_{0}
smaller enough and ensure that for all
N\leq N_{0}

\displaystyle\|u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}}+\|\nabla u_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla v_{\leq N}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}\leq\eta.

(6.49)

Here all the norms are on
([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})
.

We will show that there exists
C(u,v)>0
such that

\displaystyle\widetilde{N}(t)^{2}\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{C(u)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u(t,x)|^{2}+|v(t,x)|^{2}]dx\gtrsim_{(u,v)}\frac{1}{C(u,v)}

(6.50)

uniformly for
t\in[0,T_{\max})
. Since
(u(t),v(t)
is not identically zero, (
6.50
) is true for each
t
. Furthermore, since
(u(t),v(t))
is almost periodic, we find that the left hand side of (
6.50
) is scale invariant and the map
(u(t),v(t))\rightarrow LHS(\ref{1011s4})
is continuous on
L^{4}_{x}
and
\dot{H}^{1}_{x}
, so (
6.50
) is true uniformly for
t\in[0,T_{\max})
.

Using Hölder’s inequality and (
6.49
), we get for all
t\in[0,T_{\max})
and all
N\leq N_{0}

\displaystyle\quad\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u_{\leq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\leq N}(t,x)|^{2}]dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{C^{2}(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}^{2}(t)}[\|u_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\|v_{\leq N}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{4}_{x}([0,T_{\max})\times\mathbb{R}^{4})}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{(u,v)}\eta^{2}C^{2}(u,v)\widetilde{N}(t)^{-2}.

Putting this and (
6.50
) together and taking
\eta
sufficiently small depending on
(u,v)
, we have for all
N\leq N_{0}

\displaystyle\inf_{t\in[0,T_{\max})}\widetilde{N}^{2}(t)\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}]dx\gtrsim_{(u,v)}1.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\int_{I}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}]}{|x-y|^{3}}dxdydt

\displaystyle\gtrsim\int_{I}\int\int_{|x-y|\leq\frac{2C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{N}(t)}{2C(u,v)}\right)^{3}[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}][|u_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}]dxdydt

\displaystyle\gtrsim\int_{I}\left(\frac{\widetilde{N}(t)}{2C(u,v)}\right)^{3}\int_{|x-x(t)|\leq\frac{2C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,x)|^{2}]dx

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\times\int_{|y-x(t)|\leq\frac{2C(u,v)}{\widetilde{N}(t)}}[|u_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}+|v_{\geq N}(t,y)|^{2}]dydt

\displaystyle\gtrsim_{(u,v)}\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt.

Noticing that (
6.48
) and choosing
\eta
small depending on
(u,v)
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{I}\widetilde{N}(t)^{-1}dt\lesssim_{(u,v)}N^{-3}\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad I\in[0,T_{\max})\quad{\rm and\ all}\quad N\leq N_{0}.

Under the assumption of (
6.47
), if we choose the interval
I
sufficiently large inside
[0,T_{\max})
, we can derive a contradiction, which completes
the proof of this proposition.
\Box

Now we have precluded the existence of the two types of almost periodic solution described in Proposition 6.6 and proved Theorem 7.
\Box

7
\dot{H}^{s_{c}}\times\dot{H}^{s_{c}}
scattering theory for (
1.6
) in energy-supercritical cases

In this section, we consider (
1.6
) in the cases of
\alpha+\beta>2
when
d=3
and
\alpha+\beta>0
when
d\geq 4
.
We will introduce some notations below.

Let

\displaystyle s_{c}=\frac{d}{2}-\frac{2}{\alpha+\beta+2}

and
\hat{W}^{s,1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
be the space of functions

\|h\|_{\hat{W}^{s,1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}:=\|\langle\xi\rangle^{s}\hat{h}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}<+\infty.

Denote
\alpha+\beta+2:=p
. By the results of
[
56
]
, we know that

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}f\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{dp}{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|f\|_{L^{\frac{p(d+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}f\|_{S^{0}(I)}\quad{\rm for\ all}\quad d\geq 3.

(7.1)

For
a\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, define

\chi_{\leq a}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}1,\quad|x|\leq a,\\
{\rm smooth\ function}\quad a\leq|x|\leq\frac{11}{10}a,\\
0,\quad|x|\geq\frac{11}{10}a,\end{array}\right.

\chi_{\geq a}=1-\chi_{\leq a}
,
\chi_{a\leq\cdot\leq b}=\chi_{\leq b}-\chi_{\leq a}
,
\chi_{a}=\chi_{\leq 2a}-\chi_{\leq a}
and

\chi_{\sim a}=\chi_{\frac{1}{2}\leq\cdot\leq 4a}
.

Let
f\in L^{1}_{loc}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
be a radial function, we define the incoming component of
f
as

f_{in}(r)=r^{-\frac{d-1}{2}+2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}[J(-\rho r)+K(\rho r)]\rho^{d-1}\mathcal{F}f(\rho)d\rho

and the outgoing component of
f
as

f_{out}(r)=r^{-\frac{d-1}{2}+2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}[J(-\rho r)-K(\rho r)]\rho^{d-1}\mathcal{F}f(\rho)d\rho.

Here

\displaystyle J(r)=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e^{2\pi r\sin\theta}\cos^{d-2}\theta d\theta,\quad K(r)=\chi_{\geq 1}(r)[-\frac{1}{2\pi ir}-\frac{d-3}{(2\pi ir)^{3}}],

(7.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}f(\rho)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}\int_{-\frac{\pi}{2}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e^{-2\pi i\rho r\sin\theta}\cos^{d-2}\theta r^{\frac{3}{2}(d-1)-2}f(r)d\theta dr\quad{\rm for}\quad d=3,4,5.

(7.3)

For the radial function
f\in\mathcal{S}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
(the Schwartz function space on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
), define the modified outgoing component of
f
as

f_{+}=\frac{1}{2}(1-\chi_{\geq 1})f+\frac{1}{2}(1-P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}f+(P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}f)_{out}

and the modified incoming component of
f
as

f_{-}=\frac{1}{2}(1-\chi_{\geq 1})f+\frac{1}{2}(1-P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}f+(P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}f)_{in},

where
P_{\geq 1}
is the Littlewood-Paley operator which was defined in Section 2. Obviously,

f=f_{out}+f_{in},\quad f=f_{+}+f_{-}.

We need to introduce the working spaces as follows.

Let
\epsilon
be a fixed small positive constant and
\delta_{0}
,
\gamma
be the constants satisfying

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\delta_{0}}=\frac{2d+1}{4d-2}-\frac{2}{d},\quad\gamma=\frac{d-1}{2d-1}-\epsilon.

Denote
\infty-=\frac{1}{\epsilon}
and
2+=2+\epsilon

Let
X(I)
,
X_{0}(I)
,
Y(I)
,
Z_{w}(I)
and
Z_{z}(I)
for
I\subset\mathbb{R}^{+}
respectively be the spaces under the norms

\displaystyle\|h\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle:=\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)p}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}h\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})},

\displaystyle\|h\|_{X_{0}(I)}

\displaystyle:=\|h\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)p}{2}}_{t}L^{\frac{2d(d+2)p}{d(d+2)p-8}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})},

\displaystyle\|h\|_{Y(I)}

\displaystyle:=\|h\|_{X(I)}+\|h\|_{X_{0}(I)},

\displaystyle\|h\|_{Z_{w}(I)}

\displaystyle:=\|h\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\sup_{q\in[2,\frac{dp}{2}-\epsilon]}\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{q}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{dp}{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|h\|_{(L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{c}}_{x}+L^{2}_{t}\hat{W}^{s_{c}-\gamma,\sigma_{0}}_{x})(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})},

\displaystyle\|h\|_{Z_{z}(I)}

\displaystyle:=\|h\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)p}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|h\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\sup_{q\in[2+\epsilon,\infty]}\|h\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{q}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{q\in[\frac{2d}{d-2},\infty]}\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}h\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{q}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

7.1
\dot{H}^{s_{c}}\times\dot{H}^{s_{c}}
scattering theory for (
1.6
) with special radial initial data

In this subsection, we will establish the
\dot{H}^{s_{c}}\times\dot{H}^{s_{c}}
scattering theory for the solution of (
1.6
) with special radial initial data when
d=3,4,5
.

We need some lemmas to prove Theorem 8.

Denote
e^{it\Delta}f=f_{L}
. Let
\psi_{1}=u-w_{1out,L}-z_{1L}
and
\psi_{2}=v-w_{2out,L}-z_{2L}
. It is easy to verify that
\psi_{1}
and
\psi_{2}
satisfy

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}i\partial_{t}\psi_{1}+\Delta\psi_{1}=\lambda|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u,\quad i\partial_{t}\psi_{2}+\Delta\psi_{2}=\mu|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v,\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\quad t>0,\\
\psi_{1}(0,x)=\psi_{10}(x),\quad\psi_{2}(0,x)=\psi_{20}(x),\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}.\end{array}\right.

Duhamel formula implies that

\displaystyle\psi_{1}(t)=e^{it\Delta}\psi_{10}-i\lambda\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds,

(7.4)

\displaystyle\psi_{2}(t)=e^{it\Delta}\psi_{20}-i\mu\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds.

(7.5)

Lemma 7.1.

Assume that
d=3,4,5
,
0\in I
,
\psi_{1},\psi_{2}\in X(I)
and the assumptions of Theorem 8 hold. Then

\displaystyle\quad\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\psi_{1}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\delta_{0}][\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+2}].

Proof:
Under the assumptions of (
1.35
) and (
1.36
), similar to the proofs of Lemma 6.2 and Lemma 6.3 in
[
4
]
, we can get

\|w_{1out,L}\|_{Z_{w}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{Z_{w}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\delta_{0},\quad\|z_{1L}\|_{Z_{z}(\mathbb{R})}+\|z_{2L}\|_{Z_{z}(\mathbb{R})}\leq\delta_{0}.

Especially, for
p=\alpha+\beta+2
,

\displaystyle\|w_{1out,L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|w_{1out,L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}\leq\delta_{0},

(7.6)

\displaystyle\|z_{1L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|z_{1L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}+\|z_{2L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|z_{2L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}\leq\delta_{0}.

(7.7)

Since
u=\psi_{1}+w_{1out,L}+z_{1L}
and
v=\psi_{2}+w_{2out,L}+z_{2L}
, using (
7.6
) and (
7.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|w_{1out,L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}+\|z_{1L}\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\delta_{0},

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}+\|w_{1out,L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}+\|z_{1L}\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|_{X(I)}+\delta_{0},

and similarly

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{2}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\delta_{0},\quad\|v\|_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}}\lesssim\|\psi_{2}\|_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}.

All the norms above are on
(\mathbb{R}^{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
p=\alpha+\beta+2
. By Strichartz estimates, we get

\displaystyle\quad\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{2-}_{t}L^{\frac{2d}{d+2}+}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{2-}_{t}L^{\frac{2d}{d+2}+}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|u\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|v\|_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{2+}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}][\|u\|^{p}_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|v\|^{p}_{L^{2p}_{t}L^{dp}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}]

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\psi_{1}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\delta_{0}][\|\psi_{1}\|^{p}_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{p}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{p}].

Lemma 7.1 is proved.
\Box

Now we will estimate the nonlinear terms in
X(I)
.

Lemma 7.2.

Assume that
d=3,4,5
,
0\in I
,
\psi_{1},\psi_{2}\in X(I)
and the assumptions of Theorem 8 hold. Then

\displaystyle\quad\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3}.

Proof:
Using (
7.1
) and Strichartz’s inequality in radial case, we get

\displaystyle\quad\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L_{x}^{\frac{4d-2}{2d+1}-}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L_{x}^{\frac{4d-2}{2d+1}-}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

(7.8)

Here
\gamma=\frac{d-1}{2d-1}-
. Since
u=\psi_{1}+w_{1out,L}+z_{1L}
,
v=\psi_{2}+w_{2out,L}+z_{2L}
,
w_{1out,L}=w^{I}_{1out,L}+w^{II}_{1out,L}
and

w_{2out,L}=w^{I}_{2out,L}+w^{II}_{2out,L}
, using Lemma 7.1, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4d-2}{2d+1}-}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\frac{4d-2}{2d+1}-}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|u\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|v\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}]\left\{[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}\psi_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{q_{1}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}\psi_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{q_{1}}_{x}}]\right.

\displaystyle\quad+\left.[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}w^{I}_{1out,L}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{q_{1}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}w^{I}_{2out,L}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{q_{1}}_{x}}]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+[\|u\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}-}_{x}}+\|v\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}-}_{x}}][\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}w^{II}_{1out,L}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\sigma_{0}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}w^{II}_{2out,L}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\sigma_{0}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad+[\|u\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}}+\|v\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}}][\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}z_{1,L}\|_{L^{2+}_{t}L^{q_{2}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}z_{2,L}\|_{L^{2+}_{t}L^{q_{2}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle:=(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV).

(7.9)

Here

q_{1}=\frac{2d^{2}-3d+2}{d(2d+1)}+,\quad\sigma_{0}=\frac{d(4d-2)}{2d^{2}-7d+4}+,\quad q_{2}=\frac{d(4d-2)}{2d^{2}-7d+4}-.

We will estimate (I), (II), (III) and (IV). By interpolation, using Hölder inequality and Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\psi_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\psi_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\|\psi_{j}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|w_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|z_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\psi_{1}\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{X(I)}]

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\|\psi_{j}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\|w_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|z_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\psi_{1}\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{X(I)}][\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+2}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{X(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3}.

(7.10)

Similarly,

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}w^{I}_{1out,L}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}w^{I}_{2out,L}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\|\psi_{j}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|w_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}+\|z_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{2(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{t}L^{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3}.

(7.11)

Using Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality,

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}w^{II}_{1out,L}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\sigma_{0}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}w^{II}_{2out,L}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\sigma_{0}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\|\psi_{j}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}-}_{x}}+\|w_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}-}_{x}}+\|z_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}-}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3}.

(7.12)

Note that
q_{2}>\frac{2d}{d-2}
when
d\geq 3
. Using Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality, by interpolation,

\displaystyle(IV)

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}z_{1,L}\|_{L^{2+}_{t}L^{q_{2}}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}-\gamma}z_{2,L}\|_{L^{2+}_{t}L^{q_{2}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\quad\times\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\|\psi_{j}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}}+\|w_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}}+\|z_{jout,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{\infty-}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{X(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3}.

(7.13)

Substituting (
7.9
)–(
7.13
) into (
7.8
), we obtain the conclusion of this lemma.
\Box

Now we give the proof of Theorem 8.

Proof of Theorem 8:
By Duhamel formula (
7.4
) and (
7.5
), for any
I\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
,

\displaystyle\quad\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y(I)}+\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{it\Delta}\psi_{10}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{it\Delta}\psi_{20}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X_{0}(I)}+\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}+\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Y(I)}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Y(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{Y(I)}+\delta_{0}\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{Y(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+2}\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y(I)}

\displaystyle\qquad\ +\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+2}\|\psi_{2}\|_{Y(I)}+\delta_{0}^{\alpha+\beta+3},

where the implicit constant is independent on
I
. By the standard continuity argument, we can deduce that
(\psi_{1},\psi_{2})
is global existence and

\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\lesssim\delta_{0}.

Next, we establish the scattering result. Let

\displaystyle\psi_{10+}=\psi_{10}-i\lambda\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-is\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds,

\displaystyle\psi_{20+}=\psi_{20}-i\mu\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-is\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds.

Then

\displaystyle\psi_{1}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{10+}=-i\lambda\int_{t}^{+\infty}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds,

\displaystyle\psi_{2}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{20+}=-i\mu\int_{t}^{+\infty}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds.

Using Strichartz estimates and the discussion above, we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\psi_{1}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{10+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}+\|\psi_{2}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{20+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Y([t,+\infty))}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Y([t,+\infty))}+\|w_{1out,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}+\|w_{2out,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}

\displaystyle\quad+\|z_{1,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}+\|z_{2,L}\|^{\alpha+\beta+3}_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{[\|w_{1out,L}\|_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}+\|z_{1,L}\|_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}+\|z_{2,L}\|_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.\times[\|\psi_{1}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{Y([t,+\infty))}+\|\psi_{2}\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{Y([t,+\infty))}]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{[\|w_{1out,L}\|_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{Z_{w}([t,+\infty))}+\|z_{1,L}\|_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}+\|z_{2,L}\|_{Z_{z}([t,+\infty))}]^{\alpha+\beta+2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.\times[\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y([t,+\infty))}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{Y([t,+\infty))}]\right\}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow 0\quad{\rm as}\quad t\rightarrow+\infty

because

\displaystyle\|\psi_{1}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|\psi_{2}\|_{Y(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|w_{1out,L}\|_{Z_{w}(\mathbb{R}^{+}))}+\|w_{2out,L}\|_{Z_{w}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|z_{1,L}\|_{Z_{z}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|z_{2,L}\|_{Z_{z}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}

\displaystyle<+\infty.

Consequently,

\|\psi_{1}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{10+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}+\|\psi_{2}-e^{it\Delta}\psi_{20+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}\rightarrow 0\quad{\rm as}\quad t\rightarrow+\infty.

Letting

u_{+}=(P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}w_{1})_{out}+z_{1}+\psi_{10+},\quad v_{+}=(P_{\geq 1}\chi_{\geq 1}w_{2})_{out}+z_{2}+\psi_{20+},

we can get

\lim_{t\rightarrow+\infty}[\|u(t)-e^{it\Delta}u_{+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|v(t)-e^{it\Delta}v_{+}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}]=0.

(
1.37
) is obtained. Similarly, (
1.38
) can be obtained. Theorem 8 is proved.
\Box

7.2
\dot{H}^{s_{c}}\times\dot{H}^{s_{c}}
scattering theory for (
1.6
) with initial data has many bubbles

In this subsection, we will establish
\dot{H}^{s_{c}}\times\dot{H}^{s_{c}}
scattering theory for the solution of (
1.6
) with initial data has many bubbles.

Proof of Theorem 9:
Let
(w_{1k,L},w_{2k,L})=(e^{it\Delta}w_{1k},e^{it\Delta}w_{2k})
and

\displaystyle w_{1,L}=\sum_{k=0}^{+\infty}w_{1k,L},\quad w_{2,L}=\sum_{k=0}^{+\infty}w_{2k,L}.

By Strichartz estimate, we have for
\gamma\in[0,s_{c})
,

\displaystyle\||\nabla|^{\gamma}w_{1,L}\|_{L^{q\gamma}_{t,x}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}-\gamma}{d}}\|w_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}-\gamma}{d}-\alpha_{0}},

(7.14)

\displaystyle\||\nabla|^{\gamma}w_{2,L}\|_{L^{q\gamma}_{t,x}(\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}-\gamma}{d}}\|w_{2}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}}\leq\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}-\gamma}{d}-\alpha_{0}}.

(7.15)

Let
\varphi=u-w_{1,L}
and
\psi=v-w_{2,L}
. Then

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lll}&i\partial_{t}\varphi+\Delta\varphi=\lambda|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u,\quad i\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta\psi=\mu|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v,\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\quad t>0,\\
&\varphi(0,x)=\varphi_{0},\quad\psi(0,x)=\psi_{0},\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}.\end{array}\right.

(7.16)

We introduce the working space and let
X(I)
for
I\subset\mathbb{R}
be the space with the following norm

\displaystyle\|(\varphi,\psi)\|_{X(I)}

\displaystyle:=\epsilon^{-\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}\sup_{\gamma\in[0,s_{c}-\gamma_{0}]}\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\varphi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\varphi\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\epsilon^{-\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}\sup_{\gamma\in[0,s_{c}-\gamma_{0}]}\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\psi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\psi\|_{L^{\frac{2(d+2)}{d}}_{t,x}},

all norms above are on
I\times\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Here
\gamma_{0}
is a parameter to be determined later while

\displaystyle q_{\gamma}=\frac{2(d+2)}{d-2(s_{c}-\gamma)}.

Duhamel formulae imply that

\displaystyle\varphi(t)=e^{it\Delta}\varphi_{0}-i\lambda\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)(s)ds,

(7.17)

\displaystyle\psi(t)=e^{it\Delta}\psi_{0}-i\mu\int_{0}^{t}e^{i(t-s)\Delta}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)(s)ds.

(7.18)

Using Strichartz estimates, from (
7.17
) and (
7.18
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\varphi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\psi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varphi_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\psi_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\quad+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

(7.19)

for any
I\subset\mathbb{R}
and
\gamma\in[0,s_{c}-\gamma_{0}]
, where
q^{\prime}_{1}
satisfies

\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{1}}=\frac{d+4-2(d+1)(s_{c}-\gamma)}{2(d+2)}.

Letting

\displaystyle q_{2}=\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2-d(s_{c}-\gamma)},\quad r_{2}=\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2[1+(s_{c}-\gamma)]},

then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}=\frac{1}{q_{\gamma}}+\frac{\alpha+\beta+2}{r_{2}}.

(7.20)

Using Hölder’s ineuqlity, the fractional Leibniz rules and Young’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\int_{I}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}u)|^{p_{1}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{1}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||v|^{\beta+2})|^{p_{2}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{2}}}\right]^{q^{\prime}_{1}}dt\right\}^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\int_{I}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||u|^{\alpha}u|^{p_{3}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{3}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|v|^{\beta+2})|^{p_{4}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{4}}}\right]^{q^{\prime}_{1}}dt\right\}^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\int_{I}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||\nabla|^{\gamma}u|^{q_{\gamma}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q_{\gamma}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|u|^{r_{2}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{6}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||v|^{\beta+2})|^{p_{2}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{2}}}\right]^{q^{\prime}_{1}}dt\right\}^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\int_{I}\left[\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||u|^{\alpha}u|^{p_{3}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{3}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}||\nabla|^{\gamma}v|^{q_{\gamma}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q_{\gamma}}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|v|^{r_{2}}dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{7}}}\right]^{q^{\prime}_{1}}dt\right\}^{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{\gamma}u\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}v\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}][\|u\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|v\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}].

(7.21)

Here

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}=\frac{1}{p_{1}}+\frac{1}{p_{2}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{1}}=\frac{1}{q_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{p_{6}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{2}}=\frac{\beta+2}{r_{2}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{6}}=\frac{\alpha}{r_{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}=\frac{1}{p_{3}}+\frac{1}{p_{4}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{3}}=\frac{\alpha+1}{r_{2}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{4}}=\frac{1}{q_{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{p_{7}},\quad\frac{1}{p_{7}}=\frac{\beta+1}{r_{2}},

and recalling (
7.20
), if
s_{c}-\gamma
is small enough, the
p_{j}>1
,
j=1,2,...,7
.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\||\nabla|^{\gamma}u\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}v\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}][\|u\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|v\|^{\alpha+\beta+2}_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}],

(7.22)

Choosing
\gamma_{0}\in[0,s_{c})
such that
s_{c}-\gamma<s_{c}-\gamma_{0}
and
\theta=\frac{d(d+2)(s_{c}-\gamma)}{2}<1
, by interpolation and Sobolev embedding, we can get

\displaystyle\quad\|u\|_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|u\|^{\theta}_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\frac{d(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|u\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u\|^{\theta}_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|u\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})},

(7.23)

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{q_{2}}_{t}L^{r_{2}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|v\|^{\theta}_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\|v\|^{1-\theta}_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

(7.24)

Under assumption (C9), using (
7.14
) and (
7.15
), we have

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|w_{1,L}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\epsilon^{-\alpha_{0}}+\|\varphi\|_{X(I)},

(7.25)

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\|w_{1,L}\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\varphi\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}-\alpha_{0}}+\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}}\|\varphi\|_{X(I)},

(7.26)

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\epsilon^{-\alpha_{0}}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)},

(7.27)

\displaystyle\|v\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}-\alpha_{0}}+\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}}\|\psi\|_{X(I)}.

(7.28)

Putting (
7.21
)–(
7.28
) together, denoting
\epsilon=(s_{c}-\gamma)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{q^{\prime}_{1}}_{t}L^{q^{\prime}_{\gamma}}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]\times\left(\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}(1-\theta)-\alpha_{0}}+\epsilon^{-\alpha_{0}\theta+\frac{s_{c}}{2d}(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|^{1-\theta}_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|^{1-\theta}_{X(I)}]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\epsilon^{(\frac{s_{c}}{d}-\alpha_{0})(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|^{\theta}_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|^{\theta}_{X(I)}]+\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{2d}(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]\right)^{\alpha+\beta+2}.

(7.29)

By Duhamel formulae (
7.17
), (
7.18
) and (
7.29
), using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\epsilon^{-\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}[\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\varphi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{\gamma}\psi\|_{L^{q_{\gamma}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon^{1-\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}+[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]\times\left(\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{d}(1-\theta)-\alpha_{0}}+\epsilon^{-\alpha_{0}\theta+\frac{s_{c}}{2d}(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|^{1-\theta}_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|^{1-\theta}_{X(I)}]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\epsilon^{(\frac{s_{c}}{d}-\alpha_{0})(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|^{\theta}_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|^{\theta}_{X(I)}]+\epsilon^{\frac{s_{c}}{2d}(1-\theta)}[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]\right)^{\alpha+\beta+2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon^{1-\frac{s_{c}}{2d}}+\epsilon^{\frac{(\alpha+\beta+2)s_{c}}{4d}}[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]+[\|\varphi\|_{X(I)}+\|\psi\|_{X(I)}]^{\alpha+\beta+3}

(7.30)

if we choose
\alpha_{0}
and
s_{c}-\gamma_{0}
sufficiently small.

Meanwhile, we have

\displaystyle\quad\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\varphi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\varphi\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\psi\|_{L^{\frac{(d+2)(\alpha+\beta+2)}{2}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varphi_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\|\psi_{0}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{c}}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}(|u|^{\alpha}|v|^{\beta+2}u)\|_{L^{\frac{2d+4}{d+4}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}+\||\nabla|^{s_{c}}(|u|^{\alpha+2}|v|^{\beta}v)\|_{L^{\frac{2d+4}{d+4}}_{t,x}(I\times\mathbb{R}^{d})}.

Recalling (
7.14
) and (
7.15
), we get
